> **Executive Summary / 核心概览**: This file archives the complete, unabridged **Phase 1–132 (Priority 1–141 · 411-Question & 406-Experiment-Suite Baseline) Full Component & Phase Catalog (665 KB)** covering all **411 research questions (`409 done` + `2 blocked`), 406 deterministic Python 3.12 experiment suites, and 100+ deep-dive engineering reports**. For the fast-loading overview, 11-layer SVG architecture diagram, and 6 thematic domain tables, see **[English Main Index (`../README.en.md`)](../README.en.md)** | **[中文精简主入口 (`../README.md`)](../README.md)**.

# AI Infrastructure Full-Stack Research & Architecture Workbench

> **Language / 语言**: [中文版 (Chinese)](../README.md) | **English**

[![License: MIT](https://img.shields.io/badge/License-MIT-blue.svg)](LICENSE)

An evidence-driven, source-verified full-stack AI Infrastructure research repository for Cloud and AI Solution Architects transitioning from cloud-native systems to **GPU / HBM / NVLink / RDMA / NCCL / 6D Parallel Training / Disaggregated Inference / Liquid-Cooled AI Factories**.

Every quantitative claim in this repository is backed by:
1. **First-hand upstream source verification** (`specification` / `implementation` with exact repository commit SHAs and file paths from `NVIDIA/nccl`, `deepseek-ai/DeepGEMM`, `deepseek-ai/DeepEP`, `vllm-project/vllm`, `sgl-project/sglang`, `pytorch/pytorch`, `NVIDIA/Megatron-LM`, `kvcache-ai/Mooncake`, `ai-dynamo/dynamo`, `deepseek-ai/3FS`, `Dao-AILab/flash-attention`, `flashinfer-ai/flashinfer`, `ROCm/rccl`, `NVIDIA/TransformerEngine`, `NVIDIA/nvidia-resiliency-ext`, `SchedMD/slurm`, `kubernetes-sigs/kueue`, etc.).
2. **Deterministic Python 3.12 standard-library experiments** (`own-offline`, zero third-party dependencies, reproducible via `--check` in `< 1s`).
3. **Bilingual Chinese & English documentation** (`README.md` <-> `README.en.md`, `docs/components/*.md` <-> `*.en.md`, and 7-Card 4:5 visual benchmark decks).

---

| `flashinfer-sampling-act-qsa-rubin-mla-tilelang-phase93` | [中文](components/flashinfer-sampling-act-qsa-rubin-mla-tilelang-phase93.md) · [English](components/flashinfer-sampling-act-qsa-rubin-mla-tilelang-phase93.en.md) | [`experiments/flashinfer_sampling_act_qsa_rubin_mla_tilelang_phase93/`](../experiments/flashinfer_sampling_act_qsa_rubin_mla_tilelang_phase93/run.py) | `flashinfer-ai/flashinfer` (`fb23dd4c59`, `6964ba9178`, `f1d82ba9c8`, `0426d99a59`, `f6ba3680e7`, `95965dbbd9`, `1f24a5e446` & `f861d839e9`) & `tile-ai/tilelang` (`8f54caaa0b` & `bc0e245357`): defers `pred_lt` and `pred_eq` `BlockReduce` in `TopKSamplingFromProb` outside the 20-iteration ternary loop (**saving `38` block reductions on the normal path**); splits small-batch `act_and_mul` across the intermediate dimension `d` (**`16` tokens `4`-way split across `64` CTAs on `132` SMs, `289.03 -> 910.48 GB/s` `3.15x` bandwidth**); uses `redux.sync.add.s32` + 4-warp persistent dispatch in `cake_qsa_nvfp4` (**`1.04x` B200 eager speedup**); shaves `48` tiles off `1024`-tile 1-wave MLA rows on Rubin (`sm_107a`, `208` SMs = `50` 4-CTA clusters + `4` pair clusters) to feed `16` idle SMs (**cost `1031 -> 964`, `-6.50%`**); enables `use_2cta_instrs=1` in Kimi-K3 TP12 full-graph and MiniMax-H3 `sm_107a` 2-CTA plus SM90 DOTS3 SWA MLA (**`123.3x` lower KV traffic at `S=65,536`**); and moves TileLang `deepseek_v32` TopK scratch from `[2, 4096]` SMEM (**`28,696` overflow elements at `seq_len=32,768`**) to a `(1, 2, 32768)` global workspace while folding compile-time `cdiv(N, C)`. |
| `sglang-dsv41-vllm-kv-offload-trtllm-phase93` | [中文](components/sglang-dsv41-vllm-kv-offload-trtllm-phase93.md) · [English](components/sglang-dsv41-vllm-kv-offload-trtllm-phase93.en.md) | [`experiments/sglang_dsv41_vllm_kv_offload_trtllm_phase93/`](../experiments/sglang_dsv41_vllm_kv_offload_trtllm_phase93/run.py) | `sgl-project/sglang` (`e154c9b2e1`, `e3b713726c` & `99ca1cb9c0`), `vllm-project/vllm` (`82c42ef024`, `95a889598d`, `fd68ca9ce3` & `02186e56ca`) & `NVIDIA/TensorRT-LLM` (`2646c56fd3` & `a2a0b4a9a8`): restricts SGLang aggregated DeepSeek-V4.1 `flashmla_mega` to `attn_tp_size == 1` (`64` query heads/rank vs `flashmla` at `attn_tp_size=8`), reorders disaggregated PD coarse WAR barriers behind `inflight` processing (`launch -> process_result -> inflight -> barrier`), and adds `RequestGarbageCollector` (`MAX_DEFER_SECONDS=30.0`, **`-8.40%` P99 diffusion step jitter**); adds vLLM `sync_load_budget_bytes = 16 MiB` for inline small KV offload hits (**saving `11.75 ms` TTFT on `4/8 MiB` loads**), skips SWA eviction chunks containing `null_block` (`2` expired chunks skipped), namespaces `FileMapper` keys by `blocks_per_chunk`, and disables the ROCm AITER GDN fast path when `is_conv_state_dim_first()` holds; and switches TensorRT-LLM `DecoderRunner._start_beam_histories` to `is_last_pp_rank()` while falling back to toolkit `libcuda.so` stubs in `trtllm-render` on driverless hosts. |
| `fa4-sparse-mla-megatron-mamba-te-verl-dynamo-phase93` | [中文](components/fa4-sparse-mla-megatron-mamba-te-verl-dynamo-phase93.md) · [English](components/fa4-sparse-mla-megatron-mamba-te-verl-dynamo-phase93.en.md) | [`experiments/fa4_sparse_mla_megatron_mamba_te_verl_dynamo_phase93/`](../experiments/fa4_sparse_mla_megatron_mamba_te_verl_dynamo_phase93/run.py) | `Dao-AILab/flash-attention` (`233dfbf405` & `153df26c0c`), `NVIDIA/Megatron-LM` (`8c229bc9f2`), `NVIDIA/TransformerEngine` (`2369c39b08` & `b2c4e31e91`), `pytorch/torchtitan` (`d0c888fa64`), `volcengine/verl` (`1ff9a0a695`), `LMCache/LMCache` (`1d16a9d6cf`) & `ai-dynamo/dynamo` (`23f8028f7d` & `24221d6b80`): saves `o_lo = fp32(O) - bf16(O)` in FA4 SM100 sparse MLA training to evaluate `dPsum = rowsum(dO * (out + o_lo))` (**reducing 97%-peaked `dQ/dK` rel-L2 error `3.80% -> 0.24%`, `15.83x`**) and sets `rescale_threshold = 0.0` for `requires_grad and sparse_kv` (**`8.79x` lower row gain error**); replaces Megatron-LM Mamba2 decode chunk scan with row-level kernels (**eliminating `128.0 MiB` `z` replay buffer and cutting MMA rows `16x` `256 -> 16`**); enables `NVTE_MXFP8_PER_TILE_SWIZZLE=1` (`1 -> 32` CTAs for 4096 rows) and fixes single-group NVFP4 grouped MLP FC1 packed K divisor + FC2 dgrad transpose in TE; pins TorchTitan `VarlenInnerAttention` to `FLASH_ATTENTION`; uses blocking D2H in verl `offload_fsdp2_model_to_cpu` to prevent `reset_sharded_param` repadding corruption; aligns LMCache SWA `prefix_replay_tokens=128` full-prompt hits (`896` tokens saved); and forwards `logit_bias` to vLLM while shrinking Dynamo KV-Router lower-tier edge slots from `113B` to **`33B` (`-70.80%`, `1,077.65 -> 314.71 MiB` for 10M edges)**. |
| `flashinfer-moe-gdn-sm12x-fp4-cudnn-pagetable-tilelang-phase92` | [中文](components/flashinfer-moe-gdn-sm12x-fp4-tilelang-phase92.md) · [English](components/flashinfer-moe-gdn-sm12x-fp4-tilelang-phase92.en.md) | [`experiments/flashinfer_moe_gdn_sm12x_fp4_tilelang_phase92/`](../experiments/flashinfer_moe_gdn_sm12x_fp4_tilelang_phase92/run.py) | `flashinfer-ai/flashinfer` (`215f83cb27`, `801fcd0a52`, `55b2e33697`, `f45e1d6fdd`, `063604bfc1`, `966dcfd6b6`, `b2b2bfe892` & `90ac2eeb7e`) & `tile-ai/tilelang` (`2573d47079` & `cb26ee0faf`): overlaps Cutlass MoE 4-warp grouped activation staging on Ampere (`tile_k==128` 128-bit `cp.async` with 16B alignment check) and Hopper (BF16/FP16 issue weight TMA before activation gather, **`1.6585x` stage speedup**); skips 12 all-zero `M16xN8xK16` tiles below the main diagonal (`16*k > 8*n+7`) in SM12x GDN `64x64` upper-triangular prefill (**`32 -> 20` tiles, `-37.5%` MMA**); injects `cute.assume(divby=128)` for indexed `state` strides in SM100/SM103 GDN prefill (**eliminating up to `3.2x` slowdown**); relaxes GDN decode gate alignment to `elem_bytes` for zero-copy `HV=6` chunk views; preserves all **`7` positive E4M3 subnormal scales** in SM120 FP16 W4A16 and adds `fence.proxy.async.shared::cta` to `cake_nvfp4_attention`; zeroes `128x4` swizzled scale padding inside `rmsnorm_fp4quant` before PDL `launch_dependents`; stages rectangular cuDNN page tables in **`1` copy (`64x` fewer launches)**; and preserves signed `int64` `T1` in TileLang `AtomicMax`/`AtomicMin` while rejecting `step != 1` in `VectorizeLoop`. |

| `flashinfer-softmax-kda-megamoe-phase86` | [中文](components/flashinfer-softmax-kda-megamoe-phase86.md) · [English](components/flashinfer-softmax-kda-megamoe-phase86.en.md) | [`experiments/flashinfer_softmax_kda_megamoe_phase86/`](../experiments/flashinfer_softmax_kda_megamoe_phase86/run.py) | `flashinfer-ai/flashinfer` (`f850113433`, `f3d673e2dc`, `60d4bc1c9b`, `731ff4f334`, `8eb3006431`, `dc84070fd8`, `ff1de71bfb` & `6cc336a35e`): replaces 2-pass shared-memory staging with single-stage persistent online Softmax in Rubin `SM107` `cake_mla` (**`1.199x` mean across 39 configs, up to `1.30x`**); lands Hopper `SM90` KDA `bt16` (**`1.773x` throughput**); aligns `cake_mxfp8_megamoe_ep8` SMEM staging (**`1.119x` on 8xB200, `1.064x` on GB300**) and defers the `EP16` tail barrier (**`1.181x` `377.5 -> 319.6 us` on 16xGB300 NVL72**); enables deterministic CUDA Graph capture in Qwen3.5/GLM-5.3 FROST (**`4.496x` lower GLM-5.3 decode latency `5.44 -> 1.21 ms`**); and adds `cake_mhc` native FP16/BF16 (**`1.426x` B200, `1.384x` GB300**), 64-thread `cake_qsa_nvfp4` CTAs (**`1.168x` at `rows=1`, `2.199x` at `rows=4096`**), and `SM120` MiniMax-H3 FP8 varlen prefill (**`1.148x`**). |
| `sglang-vllm-trtllm-phase86` | [中文](components/sglang-vllm-trtllm-phase86.md) · [English](components/sglang-vllm-trtllm-phase86.en.md) | [`experiments/sglang_vllm_trtllm_phase86/`](../experiments/sglang_vllm_trtllm_phase86/run.py) | `sgl-project/sglang` (`db486b0425`, `5d11819f3a`, `640e11ebc0` & `f3ab13da1f`), `vllm-project/vllm` (`994ef140d0`, `1c3fb4de18`, `ceb01b6ddf` & `94a29d316e`) & `NVIDIA/TensorRT-LLM` (`3d816f5355`, `522daa8cdf`, `f86fc83a61` & `ecae3c97e2`): skips 92.2% of PCIe page fetches via SGLang HiSparse Top-K hot caching (**`5.125x` speedup**), hashes only uncached PD suffixes (**`4.0x` fewer hashed tokens**), narrows SM107 KDA V-tiles to `BV=4` (`384` CTAs, **`1.954x`**), and overlaps DSA CP `alt_stream` AllGather (**`1.281x`**); shrinks vLLM KV Offload high-water **`4.0x`** after 3 failures, propagates `expert_map` into EAGLE3 MoE (**`79.4%` acceptance**), recovers `PRIORITY` scheduling (`64` tokens/step), and seeds warmup caches over D2D TCP (**`37.368x` `142.0 -> 3.8 s`**); and expands TensorRT-LLM `SwapsMmaAb` mask capacity **`4.0x`**, splits MiniMax-M3 routers into 3 BF16 pieces (**`0.0` logit drift**), commits KVCM2 progressively (**`2,048x` smaller startup commit**), and fuses `NVFP4_AWQ` pre-scale (**`1.327x`**). |
| `fa4-verl-mcore-nixl-phase86` | [中文](components/fa4-verl-mcore-nixl-phase86.md) · [English](components/fa4-verl-mcore-nixl-phase86.en.md) | [`experiments/fa4_verl_mcore_nixl_phase86/`](../experiments/fa4_verl_mcore_nixl_phase86/run.py) | `Dao-AILab/flash-attention` (`1fc0221183`, `46b1bd4b6b`, `bc52a40aef` & `e7a6402a9f`), `volcengine/verl` (`f5642ca5e7`, `3c6d9b43b6` & `e1e6c3a408`), `NVIDIA/Megatron-LM` (`9c3f182e7b`, `18bc49d2f6` & `66a6d3f716`), `kvcache-ai/Mooncake` (`07829e6c14` & `6050ef5c63`), `ai-dynamo/nixl` (`5ef18e238e` & `b1c4829ef3`), `ai-dynamo/dynamo` (`93ff081d4e`) & `NVIDIA/TransformerEngine` (`39a8b879df`): halves FA4 SM100 2-CTA `hd256` backward dK/dV traffic via DSMEM K/V sharing (**`1.140x`**), zero-fills OOB rows via 5D Ragged TMA $2^{64}-\text{stride}_r$, and fits `M=64` backward in `384 <= 512` TMEM columns (**`1.241x`**); accelerates veRL Megatron→SGLang MXFP8 refit **`2.433x` (`32.0 -> 16.5 GiB`)**, single-block pinned LoRA **`5.382x`**, and VeOmni BF16 `ReduceScatter` (`-50%` traffic); saves **`16.0 GiB/GPU`** via Megatron `CheckpointWithoutOutput` and `-50%` All-Gather in MFSDPv2; and boosts Mooncake 8-NIC bandwidth **`3.081x` (`364.8 GB/s`)** + batch C-API **`12.50x`**, NIXL GPUNetIO READ coalescing (**`2,048x` fewer WQEs, `2.099x` throughput**), Dynamo 1024-slot atomic cursor (**`5,001x` fewer `ArcSwap::store`**), and TE fused ArgMax (**`8,016x` traffic reduction**). |
| `flashinfer-sm10x-mxfp8-nvfp4-kimi-qsa-ag-norm-phase85` | [中文](components/flashinfer-sm10x-mxfp8-nvfp4-kimi-qsa-ag-norm-phase85.md) · [English](components/flashinfer-sm10x-mxfp8-nvfp4-kimi-qsa-ag-norm-phase85.en.md) | [`experiments/flashinfer_sm10x_mxfp8_nvfp4_kimi_qsa_ag_norm_phase85/`](../experiments/flashinfer_sm10x_mxfp8_nvfp4_kimi_qsa_ag_norm_phase85/run.py) | `flashinfer-ai/flashinfer` (`0b352a6333`, `e7f5b1e13d`, `941fda25db`, `ee50fd017a`, `1cfd731235`, `ea95347ab6`, `20ede1453f` & `e9261a5e28`): adds $32\times 32$ narrow tiles and 4-row-per-thread programs across 6 SwiGLU+MXFP8 forms in `cake_gated_act_mxfp8` (**`1.144x`–`1.260x` on B200, up to `1.121x` on GB300, up to `1.116x` on R200**); updates 35/50 `cake_mm_fp4` programs on SM100/SM103 (**`1.077x` B200 / `1.052x` GB300 geomean, up to `1.491x`**); accelerates all 5 Kimi-K3 fused router routes (**`1.367x` B200 / `1.385x` GB300 geomean, up to `2.275x`**) and adds fence-free $K=2$ register-carry AttnRes (**up to `1.389x`**); extends `cake_qsa_nvfp4` persistent walk to SM107 within 3 waves (**`1.547x` overall, `2.526x` persistent slice**); and optimizes SM103a 1025-row N-split twin all-gather matmul (**`1.044x`–`1.080x`**), 5-deep K256 FC2 warp decode (**up to `1.089x`**), and $M=1$ fused norm combine (**`1.072x`**). |
| `serving-pd-checksum-qsa-rocm-dbo-minimax-phase85` | [中文](components/serving-pd-checksum-qsa-rocm-dbo-minimax-phase85.md) · [English](components/serving-pd-checksum-qsa-rocm-dbo-minimax-phase85.en.md) | [`experiments/serving_pd_checksum_qsa_rocm_dbo_minimax_phase85/`](../experiments/serving_pd_checksum_qsa_rocm_dbo_minimax_phase85/run.py) | `sgl-project/sglang` (`5d11819f3a`, `a887ac8fc6`, `dba22e9b68` & `0ff67ee72a`), `vllm-project/vllm` (`a55937f255`, `66bf931544`, `2c19f6793e` & `65bc698356`) & `NVIDIA/TensorRT-LLM` (`a0cee56d3a` & `9696d7433a`): hashes only the uncached suffix `[disagg_decode_prefix_len, end_idx)` via vectorized Adler-32 in SGLang PD decode (**`4.0x` fewer hashed tokens**), vectorizes LTX-2 Q/K RMSNorm+split-RoPE to 16B (**`5.793x` on GB200**), flattens QSA prefill gather (**`3.2x` faster**), and counts the `+1` EAGLE bigram boundary token in HiCache PD restores; restores 32-bit SRD addressing on ROCm `<2 GiB` `TRITON_ATTN` KV views (saving 8 VGPRs), reports the first failed DBO microbatch while preserving `[mb_start:mb_end]` routed-expert slices, and truncates misaligned chunked-local CPU offload hits in vLLM; and fixes MiniMax-M3 VL multimodal serving/KV reuse + persistent `py_lora_ckpt_source` reload in TensorRT-LLM. |
| `fa4-2cta-verl-deepgemm-mamba-mooncake-nixl-phase85` | [中文](components/fa4-2cta-verl-deepgemm-mamba-mooncake-nixl-phase85.md) · [English](components/fa4-2cta-verl-deepgemm-mamba-mooncake-nixl-phase85.en.md) | [`experiments/fa4_2cta_verl_deepgemm_mamba_mooncake_nixl_phase85/`](../experiments/fa4_2cta_verl_deepgemm_mamba_mooncake_nixl_phase85/run.py) | `Dao-AILab/flash-attention` (`47e91f1f7d`, `037e808b72` & `85d6dfe357`), `volcengine/verl` (`619c397dc9` & `8f016966e3`), `NVIDIA/Megatron-LM` (`3630e44a80` & `85bf0a667c`), `kvcache-ai/Mooncake` (`e764353f4e` & `5872ee870d`), `ai-dynamo/nixl` (`56c1c2f706`), `ai-dynamo/dynamo` (`a789babbe3`) & `NVIDIA/TransformerEngine` (`eb3c860a4d`): loads 2-CTA `dQaccum` directly from TMEM into registers in FA4 SM100 backward postprocess (**halving SMEM `65,536 -> 32,768 B`**), caps SM103 `num_regs_other <= 128`, and unifies `hd256` forward with `arrive_and_wait_unaligned`; standardizes veRL FP8 rollouts on DeepGEMM and unifies `compressed-tensors` QAT export (**`1.98x` smaller 16B refit payload `32.0 -> 16.125 GiB`**); forwards dynamic Gloo timeouts and replaces persistent Mamba residual attributes with call-local `ResidualBranchInput` (**saving `2,048 MiB` across 64 layers**) in Megatron-LM; and adds Mooncake standalone soft-pin + 64-shard per-key accessors, NIXL Trace V2, Dynamo status-only `/health` (**`14,226x` smaller `23 B`**), and TE JAX MoE FSDP + hierarchical scan. |
| `flashinfer-cake-fp8-gemm-swap-ab-nvfp4-mla-tcgen05-mxfp8-ep16-push-minimax-h3` | [中文](components/flashinfer-cake-fp8-gemm-swap-ab-nvfp4-mla-tcgen05-mxfp8-ep16-push-minimax-h3.md) · [English](components/flashinfer-cake-fp8-gemm-swap-ab-nvfp4-mla-tcgen05-mxfp8-ep16-push-minimax-h3.en.md) | [`experiments/flashinfer_cake_fp8_gemm_swap_ab_nvfp4_mla_tcgen05_mxfp8_ep16_push_minimax_h3/`](../experiments/flashinfer_cake_fp8_gemm_swap_ab_nvfp4_mla_tcgen05_mxfp8_ep16_push_minimax_h3/run.py) | `flashinfer-ai/flashinfer` (`84f3bb30b29e`, `a8ec053888bd`, `38b6198055ca`, `53ecbde224f4`, `b8ed186df31b` & `b4125448a533`): adds `swap`, `swap_run32`, and `swap_carry` UE8M0 block-scaled programs in `cake_grouped_fp8_gemm` on `sm_100a`/`sm_103a` (**`0.581x` B200 & `0.579x` GB300 latency ratio, up to `1.754x` speedup**); replaces 7 `mma.sync` programs with 35 `tcgen05` TMEM programs in `nvfp4_sparse_mla_decode(..., backend="cake")` (**`1.131x` B200 geomean, `1.152x` GB300 geomean, up to `1.375x`**); pushes BF16 rows and routes into `[16, T, 3072]` symmetric mailboxes (`6.0 MiB` at `T=64`) with direct CTA rank-barrier waits and `fence.proxy.async.global` in `CakeMxfp8MegaMoeEp16` (**`1.014x–1.019x` on 16xGB300 NVL72**, recreating session before `14,913,080` forwards); and adds MiniMax-H3 SM100/SM103 NVFP4 `256x128` N-split tail (**`1.042x` GB200 geomean, up to `1.052x`**) + SM120 FP8 out-proj (`1.009x`) and varlen attention (**`1.014x–1.055x` on RTX 5090**). |
| `sglang-mnnvl-kda-sm107-dcp-qproj-vllm-wk-cublas-nixl-trtllm-gemma4-int8-woq` | [中文](components/sglang-mnnvl-kda-sm107-dcp-qproj-vllm-wk-cublas-nixl-trtllm-gemma4-int8-woq.md) · [English](components/sglang-mnnvl-kda-sm107-dcp-qproj-vllm-wk-cublas-nixl-trtllm-gemma4-int8-woq.en.md) | [`experiments/sglang_mnnvl_kda_sm107_dcp_qproj_vllm_wk_cublas_nixl_trtllm_gemma4_int8_woq/`](../experiments/sglang_mnnvl_kda_sm107_dcp_qproj_vllm_wk_cublas_nixl_trtllm_gemma4_int8_woq/run.py) | `sgl-project/sglang` (`39b54ae0eeca`, `640e11ebc0df`, `f264333bfe1d` & `ad950cdcd966`), `vllm-project/vllm` (`2c880f1e6820`, `b3466364b69d` & `4a81a5097631`) & `NVIDIA/TensorRT-LLM` (`abb94ab8fc1c` & `64ef54ce1249`): detects SGLang MNNVL fabric devices via NVML registration state on generic-named SM107 systems, narrows SM107 KDA spec-verify V-tiles to `BV=4` (**`384 vs 48` CTAs, `13.66 -> 6.99 us` `1.954x` speedup**), replicates `128x128` block-FP8 `q_b_proj` under `--dcp-replicate-q-proj` (eliminating 1 query all-gather/layer), and guards DeepSeek-V4 MoE local completion; preserves split FP8 `indexer.wk` pairs across `load_weights()` calls, releases cached cuBLAS workspaces after `WeightCacheDaemon` warmup, and enables NIXL OBJ `capture_telemetry=True` in vLLM; and schedules Gemma4 multimodal encoder items under budget while padding INT8-woq MoE per-partition intermediates to multiples of `64` (`1856 / 8 = 232 -> 256`) in TensorRT-LLM. |
| `fa4-fwd-config-sm100-hd256-bwd-verl-thd-logprob-megatron-gdp-qb-mooncake-nixl-dynamo-te-deepep` | [中文](components/fa4-fwd-config-sm100-hd256-bwd-verl-thd-logprob-megatron-gdp-qb-mooncake-nixl-dynamo-te-deepep.md) · [English](components/fa4-fwd-config-sm100-hd256-bwd-verl-thd-logprob-megatron-gdp-qb-mooncake-nixl-dynamo-te-deepep.en.md) | [`experiments/fa4_fwd_config_sm100_hd256_bwd_verl_thd_logprob_megatron_gdp_qb_mooncake_nixl_dynamo_te_deepep/`](../experiments/fa4_fwd_config_sm100_hd256_bwd_verl_thd_logprob_megatron_gdp_qb_mooncake_nixl_dynamo_te_deepep/run.py) | `Dao-AILab/flash-attention` (`e7a6402a9ff0`, `1fc0221183e7` & `76d933102819`), `volcengine/verl` (`b1a98e1d425e` & `16ec093cc4a2`), `NVIDIA/Megatron-LM` (`b291235082b8` & `92641a433f40`), `kvcache-ai/Mooncake` (`b66bb2b5f650` & `dae22d475e31`), `ai-dynamo/nixl` (`0b146a82d8ef`), `ai-dynamo/dynamo` (`906e89d3a304`), `NVIDIA/TransformerEngine` (`c9c92255465b`) & `deepseek-ai/DeepEP` (`def865146e95`): introduces immutable FA4 `FwdConfig` (**capping SM100 KV stages at `32` within `227 KB` SMEM**), SM120 auto `num_splits=1`, and SM100 `hd256` backward `CtaSchedule`; unifies veRL THD `quant_align_size=16` and detects 1-ULP raw log-prob drift masked by `exp()`; fuses Megatron-LM `gdp_prefill_prepare` and adds global-batch quantile balancing; diagnoses Mooncake VMM/PID-namespace IPC failures and advances TENT RDMA `notify_send_wr_id_` only after `ibv_post_send` succeeds (**preventing slot `0` DMA overwrite on 255-slot wrap-around**); and adds NIXL backend phase request/execution IDs, Dynamo `LOCAL_FLUSH_TOKENS=64` counter batching (**`64.0x` fewer shared atomic adds**), TE Triton mask-map `num_out_tokens` clamp, and DeepEP V2.5 multi-buffer + DeepJIT. |
| `flashinfer-mega-moe-ll32-qsa-nvfp4-sm120-pcie-ce-ring-kimi-k3-mhc-h4096` | [中文](components/flashinfer-mega-moe-ll32-qsa-nvfp4-sm120-pcie-ce-ring-kimi-k3-mhc-h4096.md) · [English](components/flashinfer-mega-moe-ll32-qsa-nvfp4-sm120-pcie-ce-ring-kimi-k3-mhc-h4096.en.md) | [`experiments/flashinfer_mega_moe_ll32_qsa_nvfp4_sm120_pcie_ce_ring_kimi_k3_mhc_h4096/`](../experiments/flashinfer_mega_moe_ll32_qsa_nvfp4_sm120_pcie_ce_ring_kimi_k3_mhc_h4096/run.py) | `flashinfer-ai/flashinfer` (`e934818e4231`, `dc84070fd880`, `2cd4bb8c2473`, `ff1de71bfbd0`, `d7827d4e2135` & `f12321006da6`): replaces `LL128` 128B peer slots in single-node EP8 MegaMoE with `LL32` 32B cacheline slots (`28 B` payload + `4 B` flag, `87.5%` efficiency, **`4.0x` smaller peer stride, `153.8 -> 141.2 us` `1.0892x` on 8xB200 `M=1`**); stages SM103 (`sm_103a`) QSA NVFP4 page-table indices in SMEM (**`-75.00%` global index reads `132 -> 33`**, **`2.199x` `4,087.1 -> 1,858.6 us` at `rows=4096` NHD** & **`1.1677x` `11.70 -> 10.02 us` at `rows=1`** on GB300); offloads SM120 PCIe Ring AllReduce to hardware Copy Engines (`0` comm SMs, `0x88` slow-hop mask, **`1.7508x` `860.0 -> 491.2 us` at `16 MiB`** & **`1.1109x` at `96 MiB/rank`** on 4xRTX PRO 6000); and cuts Kimi-K3 attention residual HBM traffic **`4.50x` (`288 -> 64 MiB`, `3.475x` on GB300)** while staging `H=4096` 4-route Mega-mHC in `32,768 B` SMEM (**`1.3842x` GB300 & `1.4256x` B200**) with `int64_t` `CheckedMul`. |
| `sglang-msa-v1-nvfp4-kv-cp-dsa-overlap-vllm-bp-eagle-tail-trtllm-awq-moe` | [中文](components/sglang-msa-v1-nvfp4-kv-cp-dsa-overlap-vllm-bp-eagle-tail-trtllm-awq-moe.md) · [English](components/sglang-msa-v1-nvfp4-kv-cp-dsa-overlap-vllm-bp-eagle-tail-trtllm-awq-moe.en.md) | [`experiments/sglang_msa_v1_nvfp4_kv_cp_dsa_overlap_vllm_bp_eagle_tail_trtllm_awq_moe/`](../experiments/sglang_msa_v1_nvfp4_kv_cp_dsa_overlap_vllm_bp_eagle_tail_trtllm_awq_moe/run.py) | `sgl-project/sglang` (`1d9e41391343`, `f3ab13da1fa3` & `68f7f7e03165`), `vllm-project/vllm` (`994ef140d0c3`, `fc0cfcdc05b1` & `6002b2855c92`) & `NVIDIA/TensorRT-LLM` (`9696d7433a8c`, `ecae3c97e2b6` & `c81b9a28287c`): compresses SGLang `MSA-v1` KV cache to `72 B/head/tok` (**`3.5556x`**) and `1,104 B/tok` (**`1.8551x`**, **`156.04x` lower 128K read bandwidth**) with `rcp.approx.ftz.f32` preventing `~0.1%` nibble drift, overlaps DSA CP `q_nope`/`k_nope` AllGather on `alt_stream` (**`1.2810x` `3.10 -> 2.42 ms`**), and performs QSA in-place `out_proj` (**saving `112.0 MiB/layer`, `1.221x`**); shrinks vLLM KV Offload high-water **`4.0x`** after `3` failures (`100%` host-drop while exempting `p2p`), offsets EAGLE3 partial-tail proof keys (`28/40/40` tokens), and preserves `1+K=4` tokens/req in `batch_shard` (**`2.31x` throughput**); and fuses TensorRT-LLM `MegaMoECuteDSL` `x * fc31_act_scale` (**`1.3266x` `68.40 -> 51.56 us` at `M=8`**), 16B Lamport `glmDraftSampleGlue`, and `(T+1, C+1)` multimodal encoder CUDA Graph isolation. |
| `fa4-sm90-sm100-ragged-tma-m64-bwd-verl-sglang-refit-megatron-ep-recompute-mooncake-nixl-dynamo-te` | [中文](components/fa4-sm90-sm100-ragged-tma-m64-bwd-verl-sglang-refit-megatron-ep-recompute-mooncake-nixl-dynamo-te.md) · [English](components/fa4-sm90-sm100-ragged-tma-m64-bwd-verl-sglang-refit-megatron-ep-recompute-mooncake-nixl-dynamo-te.en.md) | [`experiments/fa4_sm90_sm100_ragged_tma_m64_bwd_verl_sglang_refit_megatron_ep_recompute_mooncake_nixl_dynamo_te/`](../experiments/fa4_sm90_sm100_ragged_tma_m64_bwd_verl_sglang_refit_megatron_ep_recompute_mooncake_nixl_dynamo_te/run.py) | `Dao-AILab/flash-attention` (`46b1bd4b6b8d` & `f7a38126de09`), `volcengine/verl` (`f5642ca5e764`), `NVIDIA/Megatron-LM` (`9c3f182e7b44`), `kvcache-ai/Mooncake` (`07829e6c14bd`), `ai-dynamo/nixl` (`b1c4829ef31a`) & `NVIDIA/TransformerEngine` (`a3b94812cde0`): maps out-of-bounds `row >= seqlen_k` to negative coordinates via 5D TMA stride $2^{64}-\text{stride}_r$ hardware zero-fill and halves SM100 `hdim=256` backward Q-tile to `M=64` (**`512 -> 384 <= 512` TMEM columns, `1.2414x` `3,480 -> 2,803.2 us` on GB200**); exports native Megatron→SGLang MXFP8 weights in veRL (**`-48.48%` payload `32.0 -> 16.5 GiB`, `2.4332x` `4.18 -> 1.72 s` on GB200**); saves **`16.0 GiB/GPU`** across 32 MoE layers via Megatron `CheckpointWithoutOutput`; and boosts Mooncake 8-NIC bandwidth **`3.0811x` (`118.4 -> 364.8 GB/s`)** via 3-tier HCA affinity, NIXL v6 256B SPSC ring to **`51.9M ev/s` (`4.3613x`)**, and TE Grouped MXFP8 `128x4` swizzle **`1.268x` (`94.2 -> 74.3 us`)**. |
| `flashinfer-warp-decode-k256-fc2-rubin-mla-pairpipe-mok-load-bins-ssm-update` | [中文](components/flashinfer-warp-decode-k256-fc2-rubin-mla-pairpipe-mok-load-bins-ssm-update.md) · [English](components/flashinfer-warp-decode-k256-fc2-rubin-mla-pairpipe-mok-load-bins-ssm-update.en.md) | [`experiments/flashinfer_warp_decode_k256_fc2_rubin_mla_pairpipe_mok_load_bins_ssm_update/`](../experiments/flashinfer_warp_decode_k256_fc2_rubin_mla_pairpipe_mok_load_bins_ssm_update/run.py) | `flashinfer-ai/flashinfer` (`f8ca7be895ab`, `8fd75aa702c7`, `a85950d895d1`, `a9d82c58e2df`, `6cc336a35e28` & `eb5d9fb2d236`): reduces `cake_megamoe_warp_decode` FC2 K-tile from `512` to `256` (`4` full TMA pipeline stages at `M=1..12`, **`1.05x–1.10x` on B200: `85.20 -> 77.60 us` `1.0979x` at `M=1` & `1.01x–1.05x` on GB300**); introduces Pair-Pipelined two-phase Softmax in `sm_107a` Rubin NVFP4 MLA (**`1.113x` mean across 39 configs, up to `1.30x` `262.8 -> 202.2 us` at `S=32k`**); replaces 16 fixed pair-splits in `cake_mok` with 64 cumulative-cost load-balanced bins (**`-71.43%` max bin overhead, `1.3474x` kernel speedup**); adds device-pointer scalars (`dt_bias_ptr`, `alpha_ptr`) to `selective_state_update` (`0` host syncs); and fuses TRT-LLM Gen Gated-Attention + SM120 FP8 Varlen prefill. |
| `sglang-diffusion-shm-reclaim-cosmos3-vae-dsv4-recipe-vllm-kv-offload-trtllm-retry` | [中文](components/sglang-diffusion-shm-reclaim-cosmos3-vae-dsv4-recipe-vllm-kv-offload-trtllm-retry.md) · [English](components/sglang-diffusion-shm-reclaim-cosmos3-vae-dsv4-recipe-vllm-kv-offload-trtllm-retry.en.md) | [`experiments/sglang_diffusion_shm_reclaim_cosmos3_vae_dsv4_recipe_vllm_kv_offload_trtllm_retry/`](../experiments/sglang_diffusion_shm_reclaim_cosmos3_vae_dsv4_recipe_vllm_kv_offload_trtllm_retry/run.py) | `sgl-project/sglang` (`04d01754d68c`, `3f73a3fd1cf4` & `b2c5c6db5e43`), `vllm-project/vllm` (`82c42ef02495` & `1c3fb4de189c`) & `NVIDIA/TensorRT-LLM` (`c0b33aa5e095` & `2357b058972c`): immediately calls `shm.unlink()` after worker `mmap` in SGLang Diffusion (**cutting 128-step peak `/dev/shm` footprint `128.0x` from `32.0 GiB` to `0.25 GiB`**), gathers Cosmos3 latents across ranks before temporal VAE decode (**`0` boundary flicker seams across 4 ranks**), and standardizes the DeepSeek-V4 1T MoE `1P (TP8×DP4) + 2D (EP64)` deployment recipe; passes `non_blocking=self.is_Async` in vLLM KV Offload (**hiding `81.1%` of D2H copy for `1.330x` step speedup `14.20 -> 10.68 ms`**) and propagates `expert_map` into EAGLE3 draft MoE layers (**restoring `79.4%` acceptance under `EP=8`**); and isolates late RDMA CQEs in TensorRT-LLM `TransferSession` via per-connector monotonic `(session_id, retry_id)`. |
| `fa4-sm100-hd256-2cta-bwd-verl-veomni-bf16-rs-mooncake-batch-capi-nixl-dynamo-te` | [中文](components/fa4-sm100-hd256-2cta-bwd-verl-veomni-bf16-rs-mooncake-batch-capi-nixl-dynamo-te.md) · [English](components/fa4-sm100-hd256-2cta-bwd-verl-veomni-bf16-rs-mooncake-batch-capi-nixl-dynamo-te.en.md) | [`experiments/fa4_sm100_hd256_2cta_bwd_verl_veomni_bf16_rs_mooncake_batch_capi_nixl_dynamo_te/`](../experiments/fa4_sm100_hd256_2cta_bwd_verl_veomni_bf16_rs_mooncake_batch_capi_nixl_dynamo_te/run.py) | `Dao-AILab/flash-attention` (`1fc0221183e7` & `84794dc54892`), `volcengine/verl` (`e1e6c3a408d2`), `kvcache-ai/Mooncake` (`6050ef5c63be`), `ai-dynamo/nixl` (`97761586c1f5` & `f9915d87d7d9`), `ai-dynamo/dynamo` (`662cb22de7e4`) & `NVIDIA/TransformerEngine` (`e44853058bf6`): enables SM100 2-CTA `hd256` deterministic backward in FA4 (**halving global dK/dV traffic via DSMEM K/V sharing for `1.1401x` speedup `3,120 -> 2,754 us` on GB200**) and fixes per-CTA 128B Ragged TMA slots; reduces veRL VeOmni FSDP2 gradient `ReduceScatter` in BF16 with FP32 master accumulation (**`-50%` traffic `14.0 -> 7.0 GiB`, `1.309x` faster step**); adds Mooncake batch C-API single-GIL release (**`12.50x` faster 64-key put `3,400 -> 272 us`**); lifts NIXL Libfabric's 64-rail limit to `128` rails and drains async GDS `cuFileStreamDeregister` before free; and guards TE `cu_seqlens_padded` against unsupported cuDNN padding masks. |
| `flashinfer-qsa-nvfp4-sm120-wholetile-dcp-a2a-ll128-w4a16-split-moe` | [中文](components/flashinfer-qsa-nvfp4-sm120-wholetile-dcp-a2a-ll128-w4a16-split-moe.md) · [English](components/flashinfer-qsa-nvfp4-sm120-wholetile-dcp-a2a-ll128-w4a16-split-moe.en.md) | [`experiments/flashinfer_qsa_nvfp4_sm120_wholetile_dcp_a2a_ll128_w4a16_split_moe/`](../experiments/flashinfer_qsa_nvfp4_sm120_wholetile_dcp_a2a_ll128_w4a16_split_moe/run.py) | `flashinfer-ai/flashinfer` (`83755d9093c4`, `53feaae34c0a`, `cf5c9a6a41b6`, `d08a1f732197` & `b8ed186df312`): adds SM120 (`sm_120a`, RTX PRO 6000 `188` SMs) `decode_whole_tile` for `qsa_sparse_paged_attention` when `ceil(tiles / splits) >= 11` (`704` tokens, `rows=32/64/128` at `3/2/1` splits), achieving **`1.05x–1.06x` eager and `1.05x–1.07x` CUDA Graph speedup (`100.35 -> 93.86 us` at NHD `rows=64`)**; defers the last LL128 slot release in `cake_dcp_alltoall` to the start of the next launch and writes received entries via register plain stores (**`1.15x–1.33x` across GB200/GB300 CP2/CP4: `5.584 -> 4.192 us` `1.332x` on GB200 CP2 `T=1`**); adds `BF16 x NVFP4` W4A16 Split Fused MoE (**`1.054x` `53.73 -> 50.98 us` at B300 `D1` & `1.047x` at `G8`**, crossover `0.949x`/`0.923x` at `T=128`); and expands SM103 `TMEM_NCOLS` to `480` for P-in-TMEM NVFP4 attention (**`1.02x–1.05x` on GB300**). |
| `sglang-paged-experts-uva-hicache-mamba-vllm-priority-preempt-d2d-seed` | [中文](components/sglang-paged-experts-uva-hicache-mamba-vllm-priority-preempt-d2d-seed.md) · [English](components/sglang-paged-experts-uva-hicache-mamba-vllm-priority-preempt-d2d-seed.en.md) | [`experiments/sglang_paged_experts_uva_hicache_mamba_vllm_priority_preempt_d2d_seed/`](../experiments/sglang_paged_experts_uva_hicache_mamba_vllm_priority_preempt_d2d_seed/run.py) | `sgl-project/sglang` (`2d3de82633b0` & `26a1e5110d4a`), `vllm-project/vllm` (`ceb01b6ddfa7`, `636da2476d1e` & `94a29d316ec2`) & `NVIDIA/TensorRT-LLM` (`032eb456eca0`): converts SGLang `paged_experts_gather_kernel` from 4B `words[p]` to byte-strided `row_bytes[p]` with adaptive `16B/4B/2B` copies across 12 quantization formats (**supporting `22B` scale rows and cutting resident expert VRAM `-87.5%` at `K=32/256`**) and exports `sglang:hicache_host_mamba_used_states`; replaces `break` with `continue` in vLLM `SchedulingPolicy.PRIORITY` when a running request preempts itself (**restoring `64` tokens/step across remaining `2` requests**), turns `Speculator.max_model_len` into a dynamic `@property` (catching `131072 vs 45312` auto-fit reductions), and adds `vllm preload` authenticated TCP D2D warmup cache seeding (**`37.37x` faster scale-out `142.0 -> 3.8 s`**); and gates TensorRT-LLM CuteDSL MoE async input quantization on `(do_multi_stream() or capturing) and not has_comm and not router_weight_on_input`. |
| `fa4-sm100-bwd-pack-gqa-ragged-tma-desc-verl-lora-host-stage-mooncake-efa` | [中文](components/fa4-sm100-bwd-pack-gqa-ragged-tma-desc-verl-lora-host-stage-mooncake-efa.md) · [English](components/fa4-sm100-bwd-pack-gqa-ragged-tma-desc-verl-lora-host-stage-mooncake-efa.en.md) | [`experiments/fa4_sm100_bwd_pack_gqa_ragged_tma_desc_verl_lora_host_stage_mooncake_efa/`](../experiments/fa4_sm100_bwd_pack_gqa_ragged_tma_desc_verl_lora_host_stage_mooncake_efa/run.py) | `Dao-AILab/flash-attention` (`bc52a40aef87` & `10b74d98fb26`), `volcengine/verl` (`3c6d9b43b67c`), `NVIDIA/Megatron-LM` (`ea99fdfce531`), `kvcache-ai/Mooncake` (`487da6af8242`) & `ai-dynamo/nixl` (`a9f1f7a76f94`): folds `qhead_per_kvhead` into Q tile rows in FA4 SM100 backward `pack_gqa` to accumulate `dK/dV` in TMEM (**`+13%..+26%` on B200 `hd128` 2k & `+4%..+11%` on `hd256`**, guarded by critical-path heuristics against `3946 vs 2297 us` ragged MQA regressions) and allocates `(rows // tile + 2 * batch + 1) * heads = 888` per-CTA 128B Ragged GMEM TMA descriptors (covering `582` CTAs on H100 and eliminating `~128 KiB` over-prefetch faults); stages each veRL vLLM LoRA bucket into one pinned CPU block in `lora_dtype` (**`4.71x` `165 -> 35 ms` at 4k tensors & `5.38x` `705 -> 131 ms` at 16k tensors on GB300**); unifies Megatron-LM DCP V2 train/eval schedules; evicts stale Mooncake EFA peers on reconnect during `120P + 120D` rolling upgrades (keeping AH cache bounded at **`240`**); and pads NIXL SPSC `sharedRingBuffer` atomics onto distinct cache lines. |
| `flashinfer-ep16-megamoe-relaxed-sys-route-nvfp4-mla-paced-dequant-sm120-sage-bsa` | [中文](components/flashinfer-ep16-megamoe-relaxed-sys-route-nvfp4-mla-paced-dequant-sm120-sage-bsa.md) · [English](components/flashinfer-ep16-megamoe-relaxed-sys-route-nvfp4-mla-paced-dequant-sm120-sage-bsa.en.md) | [`experiments/flashinfer_mxfp8_megamoe_ep16_relaxed_route_nvfp4_mla_paced_dequant_sage_sm120/`](../experiments/flashinfer_mxfp8_megamoe_ep16_relaxed_route_nvfp4_mla_paced_dequant_sage_sm120/run.py) | `flashinfer-ai/flashinfer` (`b743377a24aa`, `fa5c2b938de9`, `1f6e3358e07b` & `11622be4911d`), `tile-ai/tilelang` (`053fc90814c6`) & `linkedin/Liger-Kernel` (`da36c5ce2047`): replaces 16 per-peer `acq_rel.sys` barriers in `CakeMxfp8MegaMoeEp16` `route_notify_ep16` with 16 `relaxed.sys` atomics + 1 `fence.acq_rel.sys` and defers the Post-FC1 barrier past the shared expert (**`1.13x–1.18x` on 16xGB300 NVL72, `377.5 -> 320.5 us` at `64 tok/rank` hotset50**); paces `CakeMlaNvfp4PagedDecode` (`352 B/tok` KV page + `356 B/row` Q) via 16-iteration inner dequantization + `L2::evict_first` (**`1.17x–1.39x` vs TRT-LLM FP4 on GB300, mean `1.20x`, and `1.77x`/`2.65x` vs BF16**); packs SM120 SageAttention paged decode with `fp2_e2m1` (**deleting `76,563` lines, `1.38x` on GB200 & `1.42x` on RTX 5090**) + Blackwell BSA (**`5.42x` at `87.5%` sparsity**); and adds TileLang ROCm hipify header injection + LIGER 38-repo benchmark adapter. |
| `sglang-minimax-m3-hopper-sparse-dflash-dp-placement-disagg-isolation-vllm-modelopt` | [中文](components/sglang-minimax-m3-hopper-sparse-dflash-dp-placement-disagg-isolation-vllm-modelopt.md) · [English](components/sglang-minimax-m3-hopper-sparse-dflash-dp-placement-disagg-isolation-vllm-modelopt.en.md) | [`experiments/sglang_minimax_m3_sparse_fp8_dflash_dp_attn_draft_layout_vllm_modelopt_mixed/`](../experiments/sglang_minimax_m3_sparse_fp8_dflash_dp_attn_draft_layout_vllm_modelopt_mixed/run.py) | `sgl-project/sglang` (`e15d4e2d3464`, `0314ef829172` & `a2a2d02255b7`) & `vllm-project/vllm` (`fc2c21cb6d00`, `41ff22395a50` & `fc0cfcdc05b1`): routes MiniMax-M3 256-expert (Top-8) models on Hopper through two-stage Sparse FP8 Paged Attention (**cutting `S=32,768` KV HBM reads `16.0x` from `32.0 -> 2.0 MiB/req/layer` and speeding up decode `5.120x` `9.42 -> 1.84 ms` on 8xH200**), partitions DFlash speculative draft slots per DP rank `[dp_rank * draft_slots_per_rank, ...)` (**eliminating `7/8` rank collisions under `DP=8` and restoring `82.5%` acceptance**), and isolates disaggregated prefill transfers with a SHA-256 `layout_signature`; and routes vLLM ModelOpt mixed-precision checkpoints by longest-prefix layer FQN across `NVFP4`/`FP8`/`Unquantized` (**preventing `lm_head` 4-bit misbinding and `+41.8%` perplexity regression**) while offsetting EAGLE3 partial-tail proof keys by `cache_hit_alignment_tokens` (`24 -> 28/40/40`). |
| `fa4-sm100-decode-sp-pingpong-hd256-2cta-bwd-cutlass-clc-epi-verl-qat-slime` | [中文](components/fa4-sm100-decode-sp-pingpong-hd256-2cta-bwd-cutlass-clc-epi-verl-qat-slime.md) · [English](components/fa4-sm100-decode-sp-pingpong-hd256-2cta-bwd-cutlass-clc-epi-verl-qat-slime.en.md) | [`experiments/fa4_sm100_sp_pingpong_hd256_2cta_bwd_cutlass_clc_epi_verl_qat_slime/`](../experiments/fa4_sm100_sp_pingpong_hd256_2cta_bwd_cutlass_clc_epi_verl_qat_slime/run.py) | `Dao-AILab/flash-attention` (`438eb57ab480`, `ab680bd0dd35` & `1fc0221183e7`), `NVIDIA/cutlass` (`00246da882a2`), `volcengine/verl` (`226259dc67eb`) & `THUDM/slime` (`572be0b541ab`): aligns FA4 SM100 decode `should_split_kv` (`num_m_blocks * B * H_KV < 2 * sm_count`) and `128`-token SP tile boundaries, skips redundant Ping-Pong tail barrier syncs (**saving `4.2 us`, `1.062x` on GB200**), and adds 2-CTA `hd256` backward (**halving dK/dV global traffic for `1.140x` speedup `3,120 -> 2,754 us` on GB200**); defers CUTLASS 4.4.2 blockscaled GEMM `load_tail()` past the CLC loop and removes `N-1=3` over-advances on `epi_load_pipeline`; extracts fake-quantized QAT rollout weights before `_strip_qat` in veRL FSDP2 (**cutting KL divergence `70.0x` from `0.042` to `0.0006`**); and hides `94.2%` of reshard latency via Slime async double-buffered rollouts (**`1.284x` RL step throughput**). |
| `flashinfer-muse-spark-pdl-dcp-alltoall-grouped-mxfp8-qsa-sm107-minimax-tilelang` | [中文](components/flashinfer-muse-spark-pdl-dcp-alltoall-grouped-mxfp8-qsa-sm107-minimax-tilelang.md) · [English](components/flashinfer-muse-spark-pdl-dcp-alltoall-grouped-mxfp8-qsa-sm107-minimax-tilelang.en.md) | [`experiments/flashinfer_muse_spark_pdl_dcp_alltoall_grouped_mxfp8_qsa_sm107_minimax_tilelang/`](../experiments/flashinfer_muse_spark_pdl_dcp_alltoall_grouped_mxfp8_qsa_sm107_minimax_tilelang/run.py) | `flashinfer-ai/flashinfer` (`31d0a811aebe`, `53feaae34c0a`, `5396dc3b7a95`, `cc8552a3a015`, `01c9d06110e8` & `4df54c6f183d`) & `tile-ai/tilelang` (`fe6d319c78c0` & `fc0fbf0e50de`): adds `cake_muse_spark_norm` PDL (`bf16_pdl`) with $O(\log W)$ `bisect` admit index (**`1.509x` on B200 `14.79 -> 9.80 us` & `1.466x` on GB300 `13.40 -> 9.14 us`**); defers the last LL128 FIFO slot release in `cake_dcp_alltoall` and caches received payloads in registers (**`1.15x–1.33x` across GB200/GB300 CP2/CP4: `38.4 -> 28.9 us` `1.329x` on GB300 CP4**); selects `cake_grouped_mxfp8_quantize` schedules (`bulk-copy` `<= 4 MiB`, `row-tiled` `B*M >= 16384`, `row-2D` otherwise) without `.item()` host syncs (**`1.9357x` on GB200 `15.202 -> 7.854 us` & `1.9340x` on GB300 `14.950 -> 7.754 us`**); enables Rubin `SM107` (`sm_107a`) `cake_qsa_nvfp4` `tcgen05` split+merge (`num_splits=[33,33,26,13,6,3,1,1]`, **`1.33x–2.76x` vs FlashInfer ref**) + SM120 MiniMax-H3 single-pair unroll; and fixes TileLang `producer_is_single_warp` 2-phase barrier hangs plus `PipelineBufferReconciler` 4D double expansion (**`-50%` SMEM `65,536 -> 32,768 B`**). |
| `sglang-disagg-radix-checksum-ltx2-rope-vllm-mla-scale-skip-nccl-rl-trtllm` | [中文](components/sglang-disagg-radix-checksum-ltx2-rope-vllm-mla-scale-skip-nccl-rl-trtllm.md) · [English](components/sglang-disagg-radix-checksum-ltx2-rope-vllm-mla-scale-skip-nccl-rl-trtllm.en.md) | [`experiments/sglang_disagg_radix_checksum_ltx2_rope_vllm_mla_scale_skip_nccl_rl_trtllm/`](../experiments/sglang_disagg_radix_checksum_ltx2_rope_vllm_mla_scale_skip_nccl_rl_trtllm/run.py) | `sgl-project/sglang` (`ee98bb09b028`, `a887ac8fc6ea` & `933853a3f263`), `vllm-project/vllm` (`56e4e42868b4`, `c9dbc92630bc`, `0765565f103d` & `8e11a8a49e11`) & `NVIDIA/TensorRT-LLM` (`f1d8a73f640f` & `87342f453516`): restricts SGLang disaggregated Adler-32 KV checksums to uncached suffix `[req.disagg_decode_prefix_len, end_idx)` (**cutting hashed tokens `-75.0%` `4.0x` on `8K` prompt / `6K` radix hit with `0` false mismatches**), vectorizes LTX-2 Q/K RMSNorm + RoPE to 8 pairs (`16B`, **`8.0x` fewer memory transactions and `5.793x` faster `84.0 -> 14.5 us` on GB200**), and aligns GPU image preprocessing; adds compile-time `skip_scale` to vLLM `concat_and_cache_mla_kernel` (**`1.2%–3.0%` E2E TPOT improvement on GLM-5.3 / DeepSeek-V3.2**) and shares one concurrent `run_trainer_rendezvous` across dense and sparse RL weight transfer engines (**`420 -> 195 ms` `2.154x`**); and sets TensorRT-LLM MiniMax-M3 `metadata._num_tokens = live_tokens` before `_build_msa_fields()` (**preventing `45` padded slots from corrupting neighbor KV cache pages**). |
| `fa4-sm100-mla-kvhead-key-2cta-dq-reg-sm103-setmaxnreg-deepep-gin-nixl-obj` | [中文](components/fa4-sm100-mla-kvhead-key-2cta-dq-reg-sm103-setmaxnreg-deepep-gin-nixl-obj.md) · [English](components/fa4-sm100-mla-kvhead-key-2cta-dq-reg-sm103-setmaxnreg-deepep-gin-nixl-obj.en.md) | [`experiments/fa4_sm100_mla_kvhead_key_2cta_dq_reg_sm103_setmaxnreg_deepep_gin_nixl_obj/`](../experiments/fa4_sm100_mla_kvhead_key_2cta_dq_reg_sm103_setmaxnreg_deepep_gin_nixl_obj/run.py) | `Dao-AILab/flash-attention` (`94e22c906696`, `47e91f1f7dd8`, `3451a2a67a24`, `037e808b7239` & `1fc0221183e7`), `deepseek-ai/DeepEP` (`a56d6156febc` & `11d1cab604bb`), `deepseek-ai/DeepGEMM` (`78b69600794d`) & `ai-dynamo/nixl` (`d0319acbdb8e` & `c8178f563a9a`): includes `num_head_kv` in the FA4 SM100 MLA forward compilation key (preventing `(64,1)` vs `(128,2)` cache collisions), loads 2-CTA `dQaccum` directly from TMEM into registers (**`-50.0%` postprocess SMEM `65,536 -> 32,768 B`**) with `hd256` 2-CTA deterministic backward (**`1.14x` on GB200**), and caps SM103 `num_regs_other` at `65536 // 512 = 128` (**fixing `136 > 128` `setmaxnreg.dec` trap on GB300**); defers DeepEP's last RDMA `get` doorbell into `flush_async` (**`-50%` MMIO writes, `31.5 -> 28.7 us` `1.098x` on H800**), upgrades `ncclTeamTagWorld` GIN fences to `fence.acq_rel.sys`, and adds `fence.acq_rel.cta` before DeepGEMM `task_info_empty_barriers`; and adds RAII `scopedCudaDevice` in NIXL `S3AccelObjEngineImpl::registerMem` plus conditional `UCP_EP_PARAM_FIELD_ERR_HANDLER` in `nixlUcxEp`. |
| `flashinfer-nvfp4-mla-decode-moe-allreduce-union-softmax-shared-tile-minimax-h3` | [中文](components/flashinfer-nvfp4-mla-decode-moe-allreduce-union-softmax-shared-tile-minimax-h3.md) · [English](components/flashinfer-nvfp4-mla-decode-moe-allreduce-union-softmax-shared-tile-minimax-h3.en.md) | [`experiments/flashinfer_nvfp4_mla_decode_moe_allreduce_union_softmax_shared_tile_minimax_h3/`](../experiments/flashinfer_nvfp4_mla_decode_moe_allreduce_union_softmax_shared_tile_minimax_h3/run.py) | `flashinfer-ai/flashinfer` (`adca7b87d2e7`, `b2fc5958a696`, `5629a53c16c4`, `029e535ba3dd`, `b4aaeb0a11bf` & `f1d82a96fd82`): introduces `CakeMlaNvfp4PagedDecode` (`352 B/token` KV page + `356 B/row` online Q) on `sm_100a`/`sm_103a` (**`1.20x` mean / `1.39x` peak vs TRT-LLM FP4 on GB300 & `1.11x` / `1.36x` on B200; `1.77x` mean / `2.65x` peak vs BF16 on GB300**); fuses routed MoE GEMM, shared expert, residual, Lamport/One-Shot NVLink AllReduce, and RMSNorm into 2 launches in `cake_moe_allreduce` (**`76.5 -> 58.6 us` `-23.4%` `1.305x` on 8xGB200 `TP=4, M=1` & `69.2 -> 56.5 us` `-18.4%` on 4xGB300**); guards SM100 union prefill softmax rescaling with `stats_valid` (`0` NaNs when the first KV tile is 100% masked); tightens shared-expert co-scheduling to `shared_tiles <= 2` (**`89.5 -> 68.0 us` `1.316x` on GB300 `M=256`**); fuses MiniMax-H3 NVFP4 out-proj into 1 kernel (**`92.3 -> 52.6 us` `1.755x` on GB200 `M=2048`**, saving `40 MiB` FP32 scratch); and threads `max_grid_size` into `get_trtllm_gen_fmha_runner` (`148 -> 48` CTAs under green contexts). |
| `sglang-push-allreduce-norm-dsa-cp-overlap-qsa-gather-vllm-batch-shard-spec-trtllm` | [中文](components/sglang-push-allreduce-norm-dsa-cp-overlap-qsa-gather-vllm-batch-shard-spec-trtllm.md) · [English](components/sglang-push-allreduce-norm-dsa-cp-overlap-qsa-gather-vllm-batch-shard-spec-trtllm.en.md) | [`experiments/sglang_push_allreduce_norm_dsa_cp_overlap_qsa_gather_vllm_batch_shard_spec_trtllm/`](../experiments/sglang_push_allreduce_norm_dsa_cp_overlap_qsa_gather_vllm_batch_shard_spec_trtllm/run.py) | `sgl-project/sglang` (`ff4a2887d52d`, `f3ab13da1fa3` & `68f7f7e03165`), `vllm-project/vllm` (`6002b2855c92`) & `NVIDIA/TensorRT-LLM` (`263656b7d42e` & `65c47b82448c`): adds 1-stage NVLink Push AllReduce + fused RMSNorm (`Payload16B` 2x packing, `<= 512 KiB`) in SGLang (**cutting 8xB200 `TP=8, M=4` latency `18.6 -> 10.2 us` `1.824x`**), overlaps DeepSeek-V3.2 DSA Context Parallel `q_nope`/`k_nope` AllGather on `alt_stream` (**`3.10 -> 2.42 ms` `1.281x` at `CP=4, S=32768`**), and vectorizes QSA Gather K + in-place `out_proj` (**saving `112.0 MiB/layer` and `1.221x` faster**); advances `num_scheduled_tokens` by `1+K` in vLLM `batch_shard` speculative decode (**restoring `2.31x` throughput at `K=3`**); and unifies TensorRT-LLM `UnifiedKvCacheSpecs` with EAGLE3 tree block reservation. |
| `verl-mxfp8-megatron-sglang-vllm-deepgemm-refit-mooncake-xpu-p2p-dynamo-kvcr` | [中文](components/verl-mxfp8-megatron-sglang-vllm-deepgemm-refit-mooncake-xpu-p2p-dynamo-kvcr.md) · [English](components/verl-mxfp8-megatron-sglang-vllm-deepgemm-refit-mooncake-xpu-p2p-dynamo-kvcr.en.md) | [`experiments/verl_mxfp8_megatron_sglang_vllm_deepgemm_refit_mooncake_xpu_p2p_dynamo_kvcr/`](../experiments/verl_mxfp8_megatron_sglang_vllm_deepgemm_refit_mooncake_xpu_p2p_dynamo_kvcr/run.py) | `volcengine/verl` (`f5642ca5e764`, `1ff6d76ec4fb` & `c04b686a1f28`), `kvcache-ai/Mooncake` (`d4b0f357a9bc`) & `ai-dynamo/dynamo` (`662cb22de7e4`): quantizes Megatron weights directly to 32-element E8M0 MXFP8 for SGLang rollout refit in veRL (**cutting 16B payload `32.0 -> 16.5 GiB` `-48.48%` and sync time `4.18 -> 1.72 s` `2.433x` on GB200**), pads non-128-multiple vLLM DeepGEMM FP8 block-scale shards (`N_local=448 -> 4` blocks with `0x7F` identity tail), and adds two-stage vocab-parallel Top-$K$ gather (**`148.5x` smaller AllGather**); implements Mooncake Intel XPU Level Zero IPC P2P Store (`zeMemOpenIpcHandle` + 64-entry LRU cache, **`9.85 -> 1.42 ms` `6.937x` for `64 MiB` over Xe Link**); and recursively unwraps `MULTI_CONNECTOR_WRAPPERS` in Dynamo to discover nested `kvcr` secondary tiers. |
| `flashinfer-gated-act-mxfp8-ep8-megamoe-warp-decode-norm-combine-tilelang` | [中文](components/flashinfer-gated-act-mxfp8-ep8-megamoe-warp-decode-norm-combine-tilelang.md) · [English](components/flashinfer-gated-act-mxfp8-ep8-megamoe-warp-decode-norm-combine-tilelang.en.md) | [`experiments/flashinfer_gated_act_mxfp8_ep8_megamoe_warp_decode_norm_combine_tilelang/`](../experiments/flashinfer_gated_act_mxfp8_ep8_megamoe_warp_decode_norm_combine_tilelang/run.py) | `flashinfer-ai/flashinfer` (`2f911dd314bd`, `849433bb3763`, `514bb62a9d0b`, `10d7e305bb55`, `a9d16462754d` & `0840f8c2daab`) & `tile-ai/tilelang` (`82b2e9b8532a`): unifies `cake_gated_act_mxfp8` across `sm_100a`/`sm_103a`/`sm_107a` (**`4.49x` faster on SM103 `12.61 -> 2.81 us`, `1.50x` on SM100 `3.53 -> 2.35 us`, `1.12x` on SM107 + R200 `T<=4096` `1.22x` `17.9 -> 14.7 us`**); optimizes single-node 8xB200 EP8 MegaMoE by fusing `copy_to_staging`, replacing full system barriers with 1-warp arrive-only peer mailboxes (`peer_signal[rank * 8 + dst_rank]`), and inlining routing into dispatch for `M <= 16` (**cutting CUDA Graph launches `12 -> 9` and latency `165.9 -> 153.8 us` `1.08x` at `M=1`**); adds per-shape cluster Split-K `cake_router_gemm` (**`1.754x` `6.656 -> 3.840 us` on GB300 at `M=16`**); retunes `cake_megamoe_warp_decode` NVFP4 W4A4 (**`1.289x` `161.68 -> 125.43 us` on GB200 at `M=20`**) and GB300 BF16xFP4 (`1.029x` `10.860 -> 10.552 us`); ships lag-3 interleaved `cake_fused_norm_combine` (`1.018x` `30.656 -> 30.112 us` on 8xB200); and enforces TileLang explicit `target.arch` / `TILELANG_DEFAULT_TARGET` fallback before `sm_80`. |
| `sglang-hisparse-dsv4-dp-attn-bucket-diffusion-ipc-vllm-spec-autofit-kv-reset-trtllm` | [中文](components/sglang-hisparse-dsv4-dp-attn-bucket-diffusion-ipc-vllm-spec-autofit-kv-reset-trtllm.md) · [English](components/sglang-hisparse-dsv4-dp-attn-bucket-diffusion-ipc-vllm-spec-autofit-kv-reset-trtllm.en.md) | [`experiments/sglang_hisparse_dsv4_dp_attn_bucket_diffusion_ipc_vllm_spec_autofit_kv_reset_trtllm/`](../experiments/sglang_hisparse_dsv4_dp_attn_bucket_diffusion_ipc_vllm_spec_autofit_kv_reset_trtllm/run.py) | `sgl-project/sglang` (`e8211be0a862`, `fa25740754a8` & `3fd41d46e835`), `vllm-project/vllm` (`82a6f07fa9a0` & `92363c8b2259`) & `NVIDIA/TensorRT-LLM` (`263656b7d42e` & `65c47b82448c`): replaces SGLang HiSparse's fragile `numel() % page_size == 0` check with `self.page_size == 1` and token-granular `stage_page_async_d2h` (**preventing 64-token decode batches from corrupting 63 neighbor slots per page**), saves all `512` DSV4 layers (`480` sparse + `32` dense) during prefill when `enable_hisparse` is active, aligns DP-Attention CUDA Graph bucket padding on `max_bs` (`29 -> 32`), and allocates fresh ephemeral TCP ports per diffusion `Generator`; divides vLLM speculative auto-fit `max_num_seqs` by `1 + num_speculative_tokens` (**`256 -> 64` at `K=3`, preventing `ValueError: 1024 > 256`**) and calls `reset_prefix_cache()` after `quantize_model_weights`; and introduces TensorRT-LLM `UnifiedKvCacheSpecs` alongside EAGLE3 tree speculation & reserved block fixes. |
| `fa4-sm90-sm100-seqlenk-tma-nan-guard-verl-topk-cp-pack-mooncake-mlu` | [中文](components/fa4-sm90-sm100-seqlenk-tma-nan-guard-verl-topk-cp-pack-mooncake-mlu.md) · [English](components/fa4-sm90-sm100-seqlenk-tma-nan-guard-verl-topk-cp-pack-mooncake-mlu.en.md) | [`experiments/fa4_sm90_sm100_seqlenk_tma_nan_guard_verl_topk_cp_pack_mooncake_mlu/`](../experiments/fa4_sm90_sm100_seqlenk_tma_nan_guard_verl_topk_cp_pack_mooncake_mlu/run.py) | `Dao-AILab/flash-attention` (`46b1bd4b6b8d`, `fd92c6f600be` & `da6fb01fa351`), `volcengine/verl` (`30a793700e89` & `c04b686a1f28`) & `kvcache-ai/Mooncake` (`b4d9081b75fa`): upgrades FA4 ragged KV TMA from a 4D `ptr_shift=True` descriptor to a load-safe 5D descriptor with dimension-3 size `seqlen_k` and stride $2^{64}-\text{stride}_r$ (**mapping `row >= seqlen_k` via 64-bit unsigned wraparound to negative coordinate `-1` so TMA hardware zero-fills stale preallocated KV rows**), and guards softmax rescaling with `stats_valid = (old_max != -inf)` to prevent `exp2(-inf - (-inf)) = NaN`; fixes veRL Megatron CP + Top-K distillation sequence packing by advancing `pos_offset` only when `distill_loss_len_i > 0` (**`pos_offset = 448` vs buggy `704` on rank 0**) and adding two-stage vocab-parallel Top-K gather under FSDP; and adds Cambricon MLU (`torch_mlu` / `cnlight`) distributed Store support in Mooncake. |
| `flashinfer-sm103-nvfp4-tmem-megamoe-ep16-kimi-ess-sm120-tilelang` | [中文](components/flashinfer-sm103-nvfp4-tmem-megamoe-ep16-kimi-ess-sm120-tilelang.md) · [English](components/flashinfer-sm103-nvfp4-tmem-megamoe-ep16-kimi-ess-sm120-tilelang.en.md) | [`experiments/flashinfer_sm103_nvfp4_tmem_megamoe_ep16_kimi_ess_sm120_tilelang/`](../experiments/flashinfer_sm103_nvfp4_tmem_megamoe_ep16_kimi_ess_sm120_tilelang/run.py) | `flashinfer-ai/flashinfer` (`d08a1f732193`, `a4dc7d17fb6a`, `06da6cc640a0`, `506fac6203ae`, `ba12a8322385` & `4796d2effd9e`) & `tile-ai/tilelang` (`0bd33f953cf5` & `9253431f2a17`): expands `cake_nvfp4_attention` on `sm_103a` (GB300) from `432` to `480` TMEM columns (`P0_LO/HI = 448/456`, `P1_LO/HI = 464/475`) to overlap `MMA_PV(i-1)` with `Softmax(i)` (**`1.02x–1.05x` faster on GB300: `0.1145 -> 0.1094 ms` `1.05x` on `(4,8,4096)`**); optimizes `CakeMxfp8MegaMoeEp16` on 16xGB300 NVL72 via a single `fence.acq_rel.sys` + 16 `relaxed.sys` atomics per barrier and per-warp `cake_megamoe_dispatch_rows` copying each `6 KiB` BF16 row once (**`1.13x–1.18x` speedup: `377.5 -> 320.5 us` `1.18x` at `64 tok/rank` hotset50**); raises Kimi-K3 TP12 `K3_PERSIST_MIN_TOKENS` from `256` to `961` so `9 <= M <= 960` uses `k3_ess:grouped` (**`1.060x–1.080x` at `M=256, 512`**) and caches `_cudnn_version_supports_shape_override()` (`0.897 -> 0.072 us`, `-91.97%`); ships `minimax_h3_sm120_varlen_attention_fp8` (**`1.244x–1.404x` on RTX 5090**) and device-side FP64 `alpha` in `minimax_h3_nvfp4_out_proj` (**cutting GPU idle gaps `~60x` from `186.9–223.3 us` to `3.1–3.6 us`**); and fixes TileLang Ascend partial L1->L0 `l0_pitch=512 B` + ROCm `__tl_float_to_fp4` constants. |
| `trtllm-glm52-rubin-staircase-nvfp4-awq-vllm-fs-sglang-paged-experts` | [中文](components/trtllm-glm52-rubin-staircase-nvfp4-awq-vllm-fs-sglang-paged-experts.md) · [English](components/trtllm-glm52-rubin-staircase-nvfp4-awq-vllm-fs-sglang-paged-experts.en.md) | [`experiments/trtllm_glm52_rubin_staircase_nvfp4_awq_vllm_fs_sglang_paged_experts/`](../experiments/trtllm_glm52_rubin_staircase_nvfp4_awq_vllm_fs_sglang_paged_experts/run.py) | `NVIDIA/TensorRT-LLM` (`e707e62fc4d3`, `ecae3c97e2b6` & `9696d7433a8c`), `vllm-project/vllm` (`1757f6960c3c`, `503e4e16ad8d` & `4a81a5097631`) & `sgl-project/sglang` (`2d3de82633b0`, `26a1e5110d4a` & `97220dd67437`): adds GLM-5.2 Rubin (`SM107` -> `sm_100f`) swapped-MMA `glmDenseMlpSmallM` (`M <= 8, H=6144, I_local=768, 256` CTAs streaming `27 MiB` BF16 weights) and 16B Lamport `glmDraftSampleGlue` (`kNone`/`kIncrement`/`kRewind`) plus `NVFP4_AWQ`/`NVFP4_ARC` `x * fc31_act_scale` in `MegaMoECuteDSL`; fixes vLLM shared-filesystem KV offload multi-process atomic `rename(tmp, dest)` `EEXIST` (`FileExistsError`) races by treating `EEXIST` as idempotent success and unlinking `tmp_path` (**`0` false failures and `0` orphan `.tmp` files across 8 concurrent writers**); and expands SGLang `--enable-paged-experts` to `12` quantization formats with `16B`/`4B`/`2B` `paged_experts_gather_kernel` copies for sub-word scale rows (`22B` row -> `2B` copy) while exporting `sglang:hicache_host_mamba_used_states` and `total_states`. |
| `fa4-sm100-clc-2cta-pack-gqa-ragged-tma-verl-lora-pytorch-mesh` | [中文](components/fa4-sm100-clc-2cta-pack-gqa-ragged-tma-verl-lora-pytorch-mesh.md) · [English](components/fa4-sm100-clc-2cta-pack-gqa-ragged-tma-verl-lora-pytorch-mesh.en.md) | [`experiments/fa4_sm100_clc_2cta_pack_gqa_ragged_tma_verl_lora_pytorch_mesh/`](../experiments/fa4_sm100_clc_2cta_pack_gqa_ragged_tma_verl_lora_pytorch_mesh/run.py) | `Dao-AILab/flash-attention` (`98c1cee0ed59`, `bc52a40aef87` & `10b74d98fb26`), `volcengine/verl` (`3c6d9b43b67c`), `pytorch/pytorch` (`020ab1c83528` & `8e9dd78a4c84`) & `kvcache-ai/Mooncake` (`12a7e9f10a41`): converts FA4 SM100 2-CTA CLC cluster indices into logical `work_q_block = (work_m * S + stage) * C + cta_rank` (**fixing `0.39 -> 0.0` max error on GB300**), packs $Q/dO$ by `qhead_per_khead` in GQA backward (**`+13%..+26%` on B200 `hd128` 2k, `+4%..+11%` on `hd256`**, with critical-path guard preventing `3946 vs 2297 us` ragged MQA regression), and allocates per-CTA 128B GMEM TMA descriptors sized by `rows // tile + 2 * batch + 1` per head (`888` slots vs `582` CTAs on H100 `B=49, S=128, H=6`, **eliminating `~128 KiB` wrap-around over-prefetch faults**); stages received veRL vLLM IPC LoRA buckets into 1 pinned CPU block per bucket (**`4.7x` speedup `165 -> 35 ms` at 4,000 tensors `250 MiB` & `5.4x` `705 -> 131 ms` at 16,000 tensors `500 MiB` on GB300**); and sorts PyTorch `DeviceMesh` split subgroup ranks while fixing XPU `_StaticXpuLauncher` `(NO)` refcount leaks (`2 -> 1`). |
