| `flashinfer-sm100-qsa-nvfp4-rmsnorm-mhc-frost-dsa-tilelang` | [中文](components/flashinfer-sm100-qsa-nvfp4-rmsnorm-mhc-frost-dsa-tilelang.md) · [English](components/flashinfer-sm100-qsa-nvfp4-rmsnorm-mhc-frost-dsa-tilelang.en.md) | [`experiments/flashinfer-sm100-qsa-nvfp4-rmsnorm-mhc-frost-dsa-tilelang/`](../experiments/flashinfer-sm100-qsa-nvfp4-rmsnorm-mhc-frost-dsa-tilelang/run.py) | `flashinfer-ai/flashinfer` (`379c3f1c7be8`, `28b2c6d93f33`, `14df046fc7ac`, `59c07ac25a67` & `2202dd75c182`) & `tile-ai/tilelang` (`baceed6676df`, `2573d470798a` & `cb26ee0fafe5`): extends `cake_qsa_nvfp4` (`144 B/row`, `3.5556x` KV compression) to Blackwell `sm_100a`/`sm_103a` with `prmt.b32` nibble unpacking, `L2::evict_first`, non-cooperative `rows <= 8`, and `whole_tile` 2D split-K (**`1.826x` NHD `131.69 -> 72.10 us` / `1.813x` HND on GB300 and `1.889x` NHD `146.94 -> 77.79 us` / `1.878x` HND on B200 at `rows=128, tok=2051`**); replaces `cake_rmsnorm_train` cooperative launch with atomic `work_counter` chunk claiming + PDL (`griddepcontrol.wait`) reduction (**`1.168x` on GB300 `16.0 -> 13.7 us`, `1.182x` on B200 `18.8 -> 15.9 us`, bounding side-stream slowdown to `<= 1.13x` vs `10x–280x`**); ships `sm_107a` 2-stream ($n=2$) mHC (**`56 B/token` vs `192 B` `3.4286x`, `2.511x` fwd `46.2 -> 18.4 us` & `2.712x` bwd `84.6 -> 31.2 us` on R200**); supports Frost `NVFP4xNVFP4` + 10 activations (**`1.525x` MXFP4 / `1.234x` NVFP4 on B300 `T=1`**) + 128-thread Cake DSA `bwd_pre` (**`1.0504x`**); and enforces TileLang Ascend FP4 alignment (`64`), signed `int64` `cuda::atomic_ref<T1>`, and `step == 1` loop vectorization. |
| `trtllm-dsv4-rubin-mxfp8-vllm-mla-spec-sglang-hicache` | [中文](components/trtllm-dsv4-rubin-mxfp8-vllm-mla-spec-sglang-hicache.md) · [English](components/trtllm-dsv4-rubin-mxfp8-vllm-mla-spec-sglang-hicache.en.md) | [`experiments/trtllm-dsv4-rubin-mxfp8-vllm-mla-spec-sglang-hicache/`](../experiments/trtllm-dsv4-rubin-mxfp8-vllm-mla-spec-sglang-hicache/run.py) | `NVIDIA/TensorRT-LLM` (`0931dfedc9a2`, `b4bfec4baf`, `4942e2a02145` & `348347ce3e93`), `vllm-project/vllm` (`97131637e9` & `3092cf2116`) & `sgl-project/sglang` (`c677231c1d` & `5a86d6ee17`): packs DeepSeek-V4 `(global_rows << 32) | local_rows` routing counts (`61 -> 0` host syncs/step) and compresses `S=32,768` KV memory by **`70.88%` (`3.4343x`)** via `KVCacheManagerV2` head/tail mixed NVFP4 (`first_n=256, last_n=256`); accelerates Rubin (`SM107`) CuteDSL MXFP8 Grouped GEMM by **`1.281x` (`310.4 -> 242.3 us` on R200)** while fixing `2^-127` subnormal E8M0 `NaN`s via `exp2f_rcp`; cuts vLLM speculative MLA KV HBM reads by **`4.0x` (`603.98 -> 150.99 MB/layer` at `K=3`)** and restores acceptance to **`81.33%` (`+26.51` pts)**; compresses SGLang HiCache 61-layer descriptors **`61.0x` (`1,952 -> 32`)**, halves `KVShard` DCP=2 L3 traffic (**`-50%`**), and speeds up Rust abort KV reclamation **`58.57x` (`82.0 -> 1.4 ms`)**; and benchmarks the Pareto frontier across GB300 (`$0.14/1M`), VR200, Fireworks (`$0.18/1M`), Together (`$0.19/1M`), Groq LPU V2 (`1.15 ms` TPOT, `$0.58/1M`), and Cerebras WSE-3 (`2,150 tok/s/user`, `$0.62/1M`). |
| `mooncake-hca-numa-lmcache-packed-torchtitan-ulysses-te-nixl-dynamo` | [中文](components/mooncake-hca-numa-lmcache-packed-torchtitan-ulysses-te-nixl-dynamo.md) · [English](components/mooncake-hca-numa-lmcache-packed-torchtitan-ulysses-te-nixl-dynamo.en.md) | [`experiments/mooncake-hca-numa-lmcache-packed-torchtitan-ulysses-te-nixl-dynamo/`](../experiments/mooncake-hca-numa-lmcache-packed-torchtitan-ulysses-te-nixl-dynamo/run.py) | `kvcache-ai/Mooncake` (`dd1217b6c461` & `c81f4ad486`), `LMCache/LMCache` (`0d441be069` & `318d932e5bcf`), `pytorch/torchtitan` (`2fddc6b7a0`), `NVIDIA/TransformerEngine` (`9ea05cf2e8dc`), `ai-dynamo/nixl` (`c2f4580b18`) & `ai-dynamo/dynamo` (`bf115f2164`): binds Mooncake DRAM pools to HCA-local NUMA nodes (**`+62.33%` dual-CX-7 RDMA throughput `58.4 -> 94.8 GB/s`, `-54.73%` P99 latency `412.0 -> 186.5 us`**) with once-per-chain clock reads (`6.3 -> 1.9 us`) and `DeferDeletion`; compresses LMCache 61-layer × 32-chunk transfers **`244.0x` (`1,952 -> 8` launches, `4.85 -> 1.12 ms` `4.33x` speedup)**; overlaps 4-chunk Ulysses All-to-All in TorchTitan (**`-69.44%` exposed comm `14.40 -> 4.40 ms`, `1.309x` layer speedup**); caches compiled TE FP8 weights across `M=8` microbatches (**`-87.50%` casts `8 -> 1`, saving `1,568.0 MiB/layer`**); encodes 32-bit peer rank (`4.29B` peers) + 16-bit `xfer_id` (`65,536`) in NIXL Libfabric CQ data with `100%` VMM primary-context fallback; and isolates multi-tenant prefix caches in Dynamo via `TenantKey` (**`0.0%` cross-tenant hits**). |
| `flashinfer-rubin-mhc-wsmem-sm120-fp8-fa2-mtp-vc-tilelang` | [中文](components/flashinfer-rubin-mhc-wsmem-sm120-fp8-fa2-mtp-vc-tilelang.md) · [English](components/flashinfer-rubin-mhc-wsmem-sm120-fp8-fa2-mtp-vc-tilelang.en.md) | [`experiments/flashinfer-rubin-mhc-wsmem-sm120-fp8-fa2-mtp-vc-tilelang/`](../experiments/flashinfer-rubin-mhc-wsmem-sm120-fp8-fa2-mtp-vc-tilelang/run.py) | `flashinfer-ai/flashinfer` (`bf2d656f8f34`, `863a94865378`, `f1a5d2fd5396` & `a0433f8c3048`) & `tile-ai/tilelang` (`8b55d1725db0`, `f115696ba1d6` & `f53cc282589a`): adds `wsmem` persistent CTA shared-memory RMSNorm weight caching (`8,192 B/CTA`) to Rubin (`sm_107a`) Mega-MHC when `waves >= 5` (`T >= 4096` on 208-SM R200, **cutting HBM weight reads by `79.69%` at $T=4096$ (`5` waves) and `89.84%` at $T=8192$ (`10` waves) for `54.064 -> 53.064 us` `1.019x` and `110.680 -> 108.648 us` `1.019x`**); ships `SM120` E4M3-query FA2 decode (`m16n8k32` + `USE_VO_SPLIT`, **`4x` fewer $O$ accumulator registers `128 -> 32`**, `max_abs=0.0138`); auto-routes Rubin `SM107` `BatchMLAPagedAttentionWrapper` to `cute-dsl-rubin-mtp` (**`1.458x` at Graph `64/4/64/129`, `1.112x` at Eager `32K`**) and widens workspace LSE offsets to `Int64`; and fixes TileLang empty stepped loop unrolling (`extent=0, step=4`) and warp-specialized `CumSum1D/2D` `thread_offset` subtraction. |
| `trtllm-primsts-sage-vsa-sol-woq-align64-vllm-lora-sglang` | [中文](components/trtllm-primsts-sage-vsa-sol-woq-align64-vllm-lora-sglang.md) · [English](components/trtllm-primsts-sage-vsa-sol-woq-align64-vllm-lora-sglang.en.md) | [`experiments/trtllm-primsts-sage-vsa-sol-woq-align64-vllm-lora-sglang/`](../experiments/trtllm-primsts-sage-vsa-sol-woq-align64-vllm-lora-sglang/run.py) | `NVIDIA/TensorRT-LLM` (`30f1149899d2`, `576d1a4c4bd8`, `4e395f03923c` & `e10275e6efda`), `vllm-project/vllm` (`fc2e3d51f0a6`, `1e609de11a9a`, `a171c27a66a9` & `21ebaaab4cf7`) & `sgl-project/sglang` (`26365acda7df`, `40e98e1b6969` & `b503871bd76d`): ships PrimsTS SageAttention with flat sentinel scale layout `[H, ceil(T/blk)+B-1]`, `int64` `sageKMeanSum`, `_PrepareSageScales` + PDL routing overlap, `LDTM.STAT`, and short-sequence (`< 128 KiB`, `< 32` rows at BF16 `H=16, D=128`) TMA descriptor bit-85 clearing (**fixing OOB `NaN`s**); pads INT8-woq MoE `inter_size_per_partition` to multiples of `64` (**`928 -> 960` at `1856 / TP=2`**) before concatenating `w3_w1`; delegates `LoRAFusedMoEModularMethod.process_weights_after_loading` to `old_quant_method`, transfers `_lora_context`, and rebinds `moe_kernel.impl` in vLLM + GLM-4.6V full `get_rope()`; and guards SGLang `disaggregation_bootstrap_port = port` on `rust_server_serves_kv_bootstrap`. |
| `mooncake-lasthit-session-tag-te-sm120-torchtitan-nixl-dynamo` | [中文](components/mooncake-lasthit-session-tag-te-sm120-torchtitan-nixl-dynamo.md) · [English](components/mooncake-lasthit-session-tag-te-sm120-torchtitan-nixl-dynamo.en.md) | [`experiments/mooncake-lasthit-session-tag-te-sm120-torchtitan-nixl-dynamo/`](../experiments/mooncake-lasthit-session-tag-te-sm120-torchtitan-nixl-dynamo/run.py) | `kvcache-ai/Mooncake` (`575a2648f255`, `a0ff2f55ab1b` & `bb543e211cf7`), `NVIDIA/TransformerEngine` (`eb16fc57653e` & `575417c8122f`), `pytorch/torchtitan` (`ebcf95ddcb8b` & `162039e59f28`), `NVIDIA/Megatron-LM` (`88172645a9e1`), `LMCache/LMCache` (`1d1633219f26` & `c07c92a460b0`), `ai-dynamo/nixl` (`51eb5263f76c` & `84221265dd1c`) & `ai-dynamo/dynamo` (`dc673a91c992` & `a240e5c8e357`): probes Mooncake prefix keys lease-free under `LastHitOnly` then rechecks `candidate_size` groups in reverse order (**falling back cleanly if the tail group was evicted**) and embeds a 64-bit `session_tag` in `LeaseRecord`; checks TE `cast_fp8_gated` total SMEM against SM120's `99 KiB` (`101,376 B`) limit (`fwd_bf16=98,528 B` fits; `bwd_bf16=131,296 B` falls back); threads TorchTitan YaRN `softmax_scale` (`0.2266575543` for GPT-OSS-20B, **cutting vLLM-vs-Titan KL divergence `91.7x` from `0.055` to `0.0006`**, and `0.1142519529` for DeepSeek-V3) into RL rollouts; and keeps LMCache `chunk_hash` on CPU (`0` GPU contexts) while recovering sleeping vLLM v1 workers in Dynamo. |
| `flashinfer-rubin-megamoe-auto-qsa-wide-rmsnorm-pdl-tilelang` | [中文](components/flashinfer-rubin-megamoe-auto-qsa-wide-rmsnorm-pdl-tilelang.md) · [English](components/flashinfer-rubin-megamoe-auto-qsa-wide-rmsnorm-pdl-tilelang.en.md) | [`experiments/flashinfer-rubin-megamoe-auto-qsa-wide-rmsnorm-pdl-tilelang/`](../experiments/flashinfer-rubin-megamoe-auto-qsa-wide-rmsnorm-pdl-tilelang/run.py) | `flashinfer-ai/flashinfer` (`3a4e382bc46e`, `179af3f6e08c`, `28b2c6d93f33` & `75d4c6e61ddc`) & `tile-ai/tilelang` (`43f13c7ad1d1`, `f1a34efea68c` & `d85a70a97218`): adds `knobs="auto"` runtime autotuning (`autotune_v2()`) to Rubin (`sm_107a`) MegaMoE with EP `all_gather_object` workspace replacement and `_captured_workspaces` retention; dispatches `cake_qsa_nvfp4` `sm_80` `decode_whole_tile` when `rows * H_KV * splits > 2 * sm_count` & `ceil(tiles / splits) >= 16` (**`182.91 us` `1.085x` NHD & `185.63 us` `1.069x` HND on A100-80GB**); replaces `cake_rmsnorm_train` cooperative launch with atomic chunk-claiming + PDL `griddepcontrol.wait` reduce (**`13.7 us` `1.168x` on GB300, `15.9 us` `1.182x` on B200, side-stream slowdown `<= 1.13x` vs `10x–280x`**); fixes unshuffled `ds_fp8` MoE workspace (`0 B -> 24,096,768 B`); and adds TileLang 16-bit inverse math + bit-exact `hnextafter` (`0x8001`). |
| `vllm-moe-permute-rdna-w4a16-sglang-inkling-trtllm` | [中文](components/vllm-moe-permute-rdna-w4a16-sglang-inkling-trtllm.md) · [English](components/vllm-moe-permute-rdna-w4a16-sglang-inkling-trtllm.en.md) | [`experiments/vllm-moe-permute-rdna-w4a16-sglang-inkling-trtllm/`](../experiments/vllm-moe-permute-rdna-w4a16-sglang-inkling-trtllm/run.py) | `vllm-project/vllm` (`e4db46c79bca`, `96bbaa300fe4`, `2c58be22b68d` & `58301f6cb9b1`), `sgl-project/sglang` (`855c092deb93` & `9d7ad308bdbb`) & `NVIDIA/TensorRT-LLM` (`dbc875d517fb` & `461183a90a04`): unconditionally remaps `topk_ids == -1` to sentinel `n_expert` in `moe_permute` even when `expert_map is None` and zeroes padded `mm1_out` rows before per-tensor FP8 quantization (**eliminating `51.2x` scale inflation**); unpacks INT4 pairs via `v_and_or_b32 ... 0x64006400` (`exp=25`, `1024.0`) on RDNA `gfx1151` (`0.0` error); emits FP32 LM-head logits (`out_dtype=torch.float32`, **`0.5x` weight memory vs FP32 weights**, eliminating `1+1/1024 vs 1+1/512` tie-rounding flips) in SGLang Inkling + Quark block-FP8 DeepSeek loading; and restricts TRT-LLM CBTS dynamic consumers to top-level dataclass fields. |
| `megatron-mhc-recompute-stream-slice-te-mxfp8-lmcache-mooncake` | [中文](components/megatron-mhc-recompute-stream-slice-te-mxfp8-lmcache-mooncake.md) · [English](components/megatron-mhc-recompute-stream-slice-te-mxfp8-lmcache-mooncake.en.md) | [`experiments/megatron-mhc-recompute-stream-slice-te-mxfp8-lmcache-mooncake/`](../experiments/megatron-mhc-recompute-stream-slice-te-mxfp8-lmcache-mooncake/run.py) | `NVIDIA/Megatron-LM` (`c13b6e74e86d`, `a54dd7378c25` & `7d3f0fb3b539`), `NVIDIA/TransformerEngine` (`9233c73a1b6e` & `4c8044807b1f`), `pytorch/torchtitan` (`50474ad6037c` & `4103c7291ffe`), `LMCache/LMCache` (`46e33e85a56d` & `10dd1e6a124d`), `kvcache-ai/Mooncake` (`4012f2a56f69` & `d04eaf621bce`), `ai-dynamo/nixl` (`13a896bde498`) & `ai-dynamo/dynamo` (`a789babbe37a` & `2ae8a46299b1`): keeps Megatron `atomic_layer_pairs` inside a single recompute block (`1` narrow boundary vs `4`-stream wide state) and slices merged segments for streaming eviction; adds register-resident SM100+ MXFP8 cast kernels (**`0.864x` bidim geomean time vs TMA, `-15.9%` on `8160x1920` rowwise**) and fixes `exp2f_rcp_2x(254/255)` (`0x00400040` / `0x7FFF7FFF`); repins overloaded TorchTitan sticky sessions at `repin_load_ratio=1.5` (**`-8.0%` to `-13.7%` straggler time**); reports multi-page NIXL `pool.total_objs` (`64`) in LMCache; catches Mooncake `io_uring` short reads; and makes Dynamo `/health?instances=true` opt-in. |
| `flashinfer-rubin-gdn-ugpu-kcp-tilelang-ub2l1` | [中文](components/flashinfer-rubin-gdn-ugpu-kcp-tilelang-ub2l1.md) · [English](components/flashinfer-rubin-gdn-ugpu-kcp-tilelang-ub2l1.en.md) | [`experiments/flashinfer-rubin-gdn-ugpu-kcp-tilelang-ub2l1/`](../experiments/flashinfer-rubin-gdn-ugpu-kcp-tilelang-ub2l1/run.py) | `flashinfer-ai/flashinfer` (`2f158ed3c898`, `c3a7dd215803` & `0235de41d2e8`) & `tile-ai/tilelang` (`8f5129bd5a58`, `b7934818e67e` & `da680b857ba3`): optimizes Rubin (`sm_107a` VR200, `212` SMs = `2×106` SMs/die, `19.15 TB/s` DRAM) BF16-state GDN decode by cutting warp instructions **`~38%`** (identical copy-compute partition, 5-shuffle transpose-reduce, L2 `evict_last` lookahead `pf_dist = 212 * 25 // 3 = 1766` tiles) and allocating `[pool_size, HV, 2, V // 2, K]` via `CU_MEM_LOCATION_TYPE_DEVICE_LOCALITY_DOMAIN` (`ugpu_state_pool`), reaching **`88%` of `19.15 TB/s` (`516.5 us`, `1.41x`) at `B=2048` and `1.46x` (`12.3 us`) at `B=32`**; gates Rubin MLA NVFP4 decode PDL on `plan.max_splits > 1 and plan.items <= plan.units` (`54` units on 208-SM, `60` on 212-SM); ships KCP packed chunked state updates (`"wave_fill1"` `kcp_plan`, **`1.3058x–2.4679x` faster than FLA KCP** across 144 GB300 configs); and adds TileLang Ascend partial UB-to-L1 `Nd2NzGeometry`, `T.SimtVF` vector-local scalarization, and CUDA `cast_round="rs"` validation. |
| `vllm-topk-nan-aux-output-sglang-inkling-trtllm` | [中文](components/vllm-topk-nan-aux-output-sglang-inkling-trtllm.md) · [English](components/vllm-topk-nan-aux-output-sglang-inkling-trtllm.en.md) | [`experiments/vllm-topk-nan-aux-output-sglang-inkling-trtllm/`](../experiments/vllm-topk-nan-aux-output-sglang-inkling-trtllm/run.py) | `vllm-project/vllm` (`82a0a5571be8`, `73ba8f510ab9`, `02f2b4e5a971` & `06ba27b71988`), `sgl-project/sglang` (`72fc295c48c7` & `845b6be3e03f`) & `NVIDIA/TensorRT-LLM` (`30a9763022a9` & `c07c92a460b0`): replaces `__float_as_uint(x)` bit-or in `vllm/csrc/topk_common.cuh` with opaque inline PTX `asm("mov.b32 %0, %1;" : "=r"(bits) : "f"(x));` so `nvcc` cannot lower it to FP `-|x|` (**restoring `qNaN` `0x7FC00000` key `0xFFC00000` instead of `-0.0` key `0x7FFFFFFF`**), retains `EngineCoreOutputs.aux_output` across idle ticks, releases CUDA blocks in MRV2 `empty_cache`, and bounds DeepSeek-V4.1-Flash SWA replay under PCP (`window >= 2 * pcp_size`, padding Rank 0 `127` rows and Rank 1 `2` rows to `num_padded = 129`); adds SGLang Inkling `tml-renderers` token-ID output parsing and Mistral3 subclassing; and moves 3 TRT-LLM request-scoped fields (`request_perf_metrics`, `disaggregated_params`, `time_breakdown_metrics`) to Python `CompletionOutput` (**shrinking `Result` pickle state from `14` to `11` fields**) + processor `chat_template` fallback. |
| `te-mxfp8-rmsnorm-tma-torchtitan-lmcache-mooncake` | [中文](components/te-mxfp8-rmsnorm-tma-torchtitan-lmcache-mooncake.md) · [English](components/te-mxfp8-rmsnorm-tma-torchtitan-lmcache-mooncake.en.md) | [`experiments/te-mxfp8-rmsnorm-tma-torchtitan-lmcache-mooncake/`](../experiments/te-mxfp8-rmsnorm-tma-torchtitan-lmcache-mooncake/run.py) | `NVIDIA/TransformerEngine` (`1d2224e5b214` & `0b789a56804b`), `pytorch/torchtitan` (`d7f4661a9e74`, `6d4c3e268001` & `c8529e650478`), `LMCache/LMCache` (`1d7dc7c5db18`), `kvcache-ai/Mooncake` (`4759b5c627e9`, `d6f76a944a4d` & `033ca26eb4ea`), `ai-dynamo/nixl` (`de86dfed63eb`) & `ai-dynamo/dynamo` (`24f36222fb72`): fuses RMSNorm + MXFP8 dual rowwise/columnwise scaling (`128x4` GEMM-swizzled scale indexing) and drains master-thread TMA S2G buffers via `cp_async_bulk_wait_group_read<BUFFS_NUM - 1>()` + `__syncthreads()` before slot reuse; introduces TorchTitan `ActorSystem` + `RDMAMicrobatch` and passes explicit `shape=p.shape, stride=p.stride()` to offloaded EMA `DTensor.from_local`; adds LMCache `bench trace-analyze` OTLP replay simulator (`4` deduped requests, `38` tokens, `12` hit tokens at `4:16`); enables Mooncake DFS-only replica placement and frees preempted extents immediately on unbind; and propagates NIXL UCX trace context while cleaning up Dynamo restore-candidate metadata. |
| `flashinfer-mhc-tailpipe-pdl-rmsnorm-tilelang-fp4` | [中文](components/flashinfer-mhc-tailpipe-pdl-rmsnorm-tilelang-fp4.md) · [English](components/flashinfer-mhc-tailpipe-pdl-rmsnorm-tilelang-fp4.en.md) | [`experiments/flashinfer-mhc-tailpipe-pdl-rmsnorm-tilelang-fp4/`](../experiments/flashinfer-mhc-tailpipe-pdl-rmsnorm-tilelang-fp4/run.py) | `flashinfer-ai/flashinfer` (`6103f5ea7c89`, `d51e1660b22c` & `9143f50102b0`) & `tile-ai/tilelang` (`baceed6676df`, `eaa9468d60f2` & `876a59e6eb80`): dispatches the fold-overlapped `"tailpipe"` kernel (`cake_mega_mhc_c549b064530c3765f9ba`) on `sm_107a` when `mhc_infer_tail_splits <= 22` (`T >= 385` on 208-SM R200, **`1.009x–1.024x` speedup on R200: `55.512 -> 54.208 us` at `T=4096` and `112.656 -> 111.103 us` at `T=8192`**); removes 6 unused headers from `norm.cuh` and proves across `242` GH200 PDL RMSNorm tests that removing `cudaGridDependencySynchronize()` from 2D norm kernels **fails `56/66` race cases (`100%` `33,792/33,792` corrupted elements on `264x128`)**; folds `43` XQA + `7` GQA Decode `sm_110a` (Thor) units into `9` headers (**`-14,131` net lines, `50/50` bit-identical SASS**); and generalizes TileLang Ascend packed FP4 MN-major alignment to `256 // operand_bits = 64`, inserts `1` same-iteration WAW barrier on overlapping shared writes (`0` on disjoint), and requires explicit K padding for unaligned CUDA MMA chunks. |
| `sglang-response-template-logits-trtllm-cosmos3-edge` | [中文](components/sglang-response-template-logits-trtllm-cosmos3-edge.md) · [English](components/sglang-response-template-logits-trtllm-cosmos3-edge.en.md) | [`experiments/sglang-response-template-logits-trtllm-cosmos3-edge/`](../experiments/sglang-response-template-logits-trtllm-cosmos3-edge/run.py) | `sgl-project/sglang` (`27ac614f051f`, `f05ad5ec0139` & `baf80c61b7ea`), `NVIDIA/TensorRT-LLM` (`39a44fab74f4`) & `vllm-project/vllm` (`e2b696312328`, `1b99dc5e8fce` & `5c465dda9da3`): introduces checkpoint-driven `response_template` serving adapters (`4` derived template variants, preserving `tool_close_token_ids` on thinking stops and switching post-reasoning JSON-schema tokens to `_verbatim=True`), replaces `logits_buffer.copy_(logits)` with Triton `cast_to_float32_logits` (**`2.8x` faster BF16->FP32 cast on GB300, `55.69 MiB` traffic at `[64, 152064]`**), and binds out-of-tree platforms via `GroupCoordinator` device + `"PrivateUse1"` custom ops; adds `Cosmos3EdgeForConditionalGeneration` to TensorRT-LLM (interleaved mRoPE `[24,20,20]` + `Cosmos3EdgeHfWeightMapper` filtering `17` diffusion-generator regexes); and emits vLLM streaming `kv_transfer_params` only on terminal `finish_reason` chunks while normalizing Rust `""` tool arguments to `"{}"` and reporting all invalid `logit_bias` keys. |
| `te-wide-mxfp8-requant-lmcache-addr-nixl-ucx-notif` | [中文](components/te-wide-mxfp8-requant-lmcache-addr-nixl-ucx-notif.md) · [English](components/te-wide-mxfp8-requant-lmcache-addr-nixl-ucx-notif.en.md) | [`experiments/te-wide-mxfp8-requant-lmcache-addr-nixl-ucx-notif/`](../experiments/te-wide-mxfp8-requant-lmcache-addr-nixl-ucx-notif/run.py) | `NVIDIA/TransformerEngine` (`15dcc848ecff` & `dd02f3f77c49`), `LMCache/LMCache` (`318d932e5bcf`), `ai-dynamo/nixl` (`01656769641d`), `pytorch/torchtitan` (`323cf2b49646` & `3c7c1479151a`), `NVIDIA/Megatron-LM` (`888887d35e37`), `kvcache-ai/Mooncake` (`f515971c9aaa`) & `ai-dynamo/dynamo` (`a0de541ea8c8`): adds SM100+ `128x128` wide-tile grouped MXFP8 requantization (**`4x` fewer tiles than `32x128`, coalesced `512 B` swizzled scale writes, and persistent `live_end` early exit running `512/4096 = 12.5%` tiles on a `2048`-row live prefix**); centralizes LMCache `transfer_with_address_kernel` (`MAX_CHUNKS_PER_LAUNCH = 4`, launch-0 `skip_prefix_n_blocks`, and pinned staging + `synchronize_device` for unpinned chunks); uses `nixlSerDes::exportMoveStr() &&` and `absl::InlinedVector` in NIXL UCX notifications (**cutting avg prep latency `-32.14%` `2.8 -> 1.9 us` and P99 post `-25.0%` `12.0 -> 9.0 us`**); and fixes TorchTitan Flex `FX_ANNOTATION` per-layer recompiles (`14 -> 1` graph, exact `6.9951515` loss), 4-tuple PP unpack, RL `GenerationEngine` split, Mixtral Transformers 5.0.0 compatibility, Mooncake DFS KV bench, and Dynamo `DYN_DISABLE_OPENAI_STRICT_SCHEMA_VALIDATION`. |
| `flashinfer-cake-gdp-2head-cold-chain-frost-ffi` | [中文](components/flashinfer-cake-gdp-2head-cold-chain-frost-ffi.md) · [English](components/flashinfer-cake-gdp-2head-cold-chain-frost-ffi.en.md) | [`experiments/flashinfer-cake-gdp-2head-cold-chain-frost-ffi/`](../experiments/flashinfer-cake-gdp-2head-cold-chain-frost-ffi/run.py) | `flashinfer-ai/flashinfer` (`58232f576ad7`, `aec44bda63e6` & `c0e51ae29b19`) & `tile-ai/tilelang` (`cb26ee0fafe5` & `2573d470798a`): dispatches two-heads-per-CTA `T=1` decode (`l8r4x2`, `(B*HV+1)//2` CTAs when `B*HV >= 32*sm_count`, **`1.18x` on B200 `243.4 -> 206.1 us` and `1.13x` on GB300 `228.0 -> 201.7 us` at `HV=80, B=256, T=1`**) and replaces dual 5-step warp butterfly reductions in `splitv8_tile8` with paired-row half-warp reduce-scatter + all-gather (**cutting `__shfl_xor_sync` calls per substep `10 -> 6`, `-40.0%`**); chains `L=8` distinct `ValueProfileArena` slots per L2 flush under `cold-chain:8` (**amortizing B300's `6.38 us` single-launch floor to pick `SplitK(64,8,4,12)` at `3.74 us` over `WarpSplitK` at `5.17 us` and dropping `pdl=1` `>5%` misses `21/30 -> 1/30`**); compiles one `@cute.jit` TVM-FFI `launch` entry per Frost tactic (**`4.22x` public API `260.85 -> 61.85 us` and `8.75x` runner `217.82 -> 24.90 us` on B300 `T=1` `MXFP4 TC128`**); and rejects `step != 1` in TileLang `VectorizeLoop` while preserving signed `int64` `T1` in `cuda::atomic_ref`. |
| `sglang-glm53-cp-bcg-trtllm-kda-conv-vllm-gfx942` | [中文](components/sglang-glm53-cp-bcg-trtllm-kda-conv-vllm-gfx942.md) · [English](components/sglang-glm53-cp-bcg-trtllm-kda-conv-vllm-gfx942.en.md) | [`experiments/sglang-glm53-cp-bcg-trtllm-kda-conv-vllm-gfx942/`](../experiments/sglang-glm53-cp-bcg-trtllm-kda-conv-vllm-gfx942/run.py) | `NVIDIA/TensorRT-LLM` (`222676c8d7bd` & `4ccdca1e8492`), `sgl-project/sglang` (`ea226cf78267`, `5f7542d7627f` & `15dc838f9bf5`) & `vllm-project/vllm` (`18df4cd9a98b` & `29cf023770a0`): fuses CuTe-DSL KDA prefill short conv + indexed state pool update (`-7,214` legacy lines) with a 1-warp-vote guard for `min(h) < -2.0` on fast `silu(x) = h + h * tanh.approx(h)` (**bounding error to `< 1.35%` for $h \ge -2$ while preventing `>3953%` cancellation at $h=-6$**) and widens GVR v2 Top-K `QC` `96 -> 160` while saving **`4,096 B` SMEM/CTA** at `NBH=1024`; separates static `graph_num_tokens`, global `cache_tokens`, and scalar `moe_local_token_count` in SGLang GLM-5.3 Flash padded CP BCG, caches DSV4.1 MegaGate `(valid, image)` row masks once per forward (**`120 -> 2` mask ops across 60 MoE layers, `60.0x` reduction**), and maps `SGLANG_TIMEOUT_KEEP_ALIVE` (`s -> ms`) in Granian HTTP/1.1; and keeps ROCm `gfx942` PagedAttention head offset in 32-bit `int32` (**eliminating SGPR spill `NaN`s**) while unifying `gfx950` FlyDSL `GQA-{6,8,16}` (`-875` lines + `base_byte_offset`). |
| `megatron-mfsdp-mxfp8-planes-lmcache-lookup-nixl-ring` | [中文](components/megatron-mfsdp-mxfp8-planes-lmcache-lookup-nixl-ring.md) · [English](components/megatron-mfsdp-mxfp8-planes-lmcache-lookup-nixl-ring.en.md) | [`experiments/megatron-mfsdp-mxfp8-planes-lmcache-lookup-nixl-ring/`](../experiments/megatron-mfsdp-mxfp8-planes-lmcache-lookup-nixl-ring/run.py) | `NVIDIA/Megatron-LM` (`18bc49d2f6bc`), `NVIDIA/TransformerEngine` (`c9c92255465b` & `2199b7166417`), `pytorch/torchtitan` (`43d1bb98014e` & `d696f48b43ad`), `LMCache/LMCache` (`ff9185d419e1` & `a81b4e5aea0c`), `kvcache-ai/Mooncake` (`dd1217b6c461`), `ai-dynamo/nixl` (`a9f1f7a76f46`) & `ai-dynamo/dynamo` (`906e89d3a304` & `45cc9e8a1a9e`): gathers only rowwise MXFP8 planes in `Phase.FORWARD` and columnwise planes in non-recompute `Phase.BACKWARD` (**cutting per-phase All-Gather payload by `50.0%`, `34.60 -> 17.30 MB` on `4096x4096`**) and clamps over-capacity TE Triton `row_id >= num_out_tokens` to `-1`; reconfigures TorchTitan Muse Spark 816B-A42B GB300 RL trainer from `256` to **`128` GPUs (`FSDP=8 x TP=4 x CP=4 x EP=16`, `8.0x` smaller expert FSDP group, `28%` MFU at `131K`)** and passes packed `2x2` sequence length (`256` vs `1024` at 256px) to Flux `get_schedule`; frees excess LMCache lookup tail locks in `[min_hits, server_hits)`; reads `clock_gettime` once per Mooncake RDMA post chain (**cutting 256-WR lock hold `6.3 -> 1.9 us`, `-69.84%`**); and aligns NIXL ring headers to `256 B` (`512 B` total) while batching Dynamo output-token atomics every `64` tokens (**`64.0x` reduction**). |
| `flashinfer-cudnn-prefill-splitk-fence-tilelang-nvrtc` | [中文](components/flashinfer-cudnn-prefill-splitk-fence-tilelang-nvrtc.md) · [English](components/flashinfer-cudnn-prefill-splitk-fence-tilelang-nvrtc.en.md) | [`experiments/flashinfer-cudnn-prefill-splitk-fence-tilelang-nvrtc/`](../experiments/flashinfer-cudnn-prefill-splitk-fence-tilelang-nvrtc/run.py) | `flashinfer-ai/flashinfer` (`b27f6cae7da4`, `c56cd4fb7222`, `c01aa7779bbb` & `6b38488084e3`) & `tile-ai/tilelang` (`35ee29507557` & `fe8c34618ffb`): checks scalar invariants and compares private detached views (`self.inputs`) with immutable `dataclasses.replace(metadata, ...)` in `_CudnnPrefillPlan.prepare()` (**cutting H100 NVL host `plan()` latency by `13.90%` `79.12 -> 68.12 us` at `B1,Q128,KV8192` and `14.93%` `77.38 -> 65.83 us` at `B4,Q512,KV512` (`plan+run` `-9.80%`), while catching in-place `as_strided_((2,4),(1,2))` mutations on retained metadata**); inserts `fence.proxy.async.shared::cta;` across 6 split-K slab collectors (`_sks_sb2`) and refreshes `st.global.L1::no_allocate.L2::evict_first.v8.b32` lowerings across `247` `sm_100a`/`103a`/`107a` build entries (**`1.008x–1.016x` GB300 & `1.003x–1.037x` GB200**); folds 3 FP32 wgrad MoE clones via `-DWGRAD_OUT=32` (`8 -> 5` programs, **`-4,954` combined net lines**, bitwise-identical SASS across `26` libraries); and fixes TileLang `NVRTCAdapter.from_database` `libpath` + `VectorizeLoop` `analyzer->Clone()`. |
| `sglang-gguf-moe-trtllm-mxfp8-e8m0-vllm-shared-rope` | [中文](components/sglang-gguf-moe-trtllm-mxfp8-e8m0-vllm-shared-rope.md) · [English](components/sglang-gguf-moe-trtllm-mxfp8-e8m0-vllm-shared-rope.en.md) | [`experiments/sglang-gguf-moe-trtllm-mxfp8-e8m0-vllm-shared-rope/`](../experiments/sglang-gguf-moe-trtllm-mxfp8-e8m0-vllm-shared-rope/run.py) | `NVIDIA/TensorRT-LLM` (`348347ce3e93`, `4942e2a02145` & `49bd803025eb`), `sgl-project/sglang` (`e6451c1a956d` & `194abb8e54a8`) & `vllm-project/vllm` (`b1c66e55453d` & `d143a9de3915`): replaces `reciprocal_approximate_ftz(SFValue)` (`rcp.approx.ftz.f32`) with exact `exp2f_rcp(fp8SFVal)` in `cvt_warp_fp16_to_mxfp8` (**returning finite normal $2^{127}$ for subnormal `E8M0` scale $2^{-127}$ and eliminating `0.0 * inf = NaN` in near-zero blocks `vecMax in (0, 448 * 2^-127]`**), reads `mapping.world_size` under Ray (`TLLM_DISABLE_MPI`), and gates PWCG NVTX probes on `TLLM_PWCG_NVTX_PROBE=1`; loops `for (int i = 0; i < mmq_x; i += nwarps)` in SGLang GGUF `moe_q` (**loading all `64/64` rows of `tile_y_ds` instead of `8/64` at `nwarps=8`**), uses PTX `mul.rn.f32` to preserve subnormals under SM90 `-use_fast_math`, and synchronizes `self.stream` after `self.outputs.clear()` in Cosmos Dreams `CausalDecodeQueue.__exit__`; and shares a single `get_rope()` cache bounded to `max_model_len=8192` across 32 ROCm Muse Spark layers in vLLM (**`2,048.0 -> 4.0 MiB`, `512.0x` reduction**) + honors `VLLM_WORKER_SHUTDOWN_TIMEOUT_SECONDS` on immediate aborts. |
| `megatron-padding-zero-lmcache-swa-replay-mooncake-wrid` | [中文](components/megatron-padding-zero-lmcache-swa-replay-mooncake-wrid.md) · [English](components/megatron-padding-zero-lmcache-swa-replay-mooncake-wrid.en.md) | [`experiments/megatron-padding-zero-lmcache-swa-replay-mooncake-wrid/`](../experiments/megatron-padding-zero-lmcache-swa-replay-mooncake-wrid/run.py) | `NVIDIA/Megatron-LM` (`f9d9bdcc6e09` & `92641a433f40`), `NVIDIA/TransformerEngine` (`9ea05cf2e8dc`), `LMCache/LMCache` (`a0ff4701284d`), `kvcache-ai/Mooncake` (`dae22d475e31` & `b66bb2b5f650`) & `ai-dynamo/nixl` (`f3673f6be3f4` & `70badd6aad36`): zeroes padded token rows from the `[1]` `int32` device tensor `gpu_view.real_token_count` during CUDA-graph replay in `zero_padding_rows_kernel.py` (**recovering `32.0x` FP8 scale precision `7.0 -> 224.0` when `72/128` rows are real**) and pools global-batch K3 `qb_histogram`; enables FP8 weight workspace caching in `torch.compile`d TE `Linear` (**cutting 8-microbatch step weight casts `8 -> 1`, `-87.50%`, peak memory `452 MiB` eager / `539 MiB` compiled**); aligns LMCache SWA full-prompt prefix replay (`12 == 12`) to chunk boundaries (`(12-1)//8*8 = 8`, unlocking `[8, 12)` and **eliminating 4-token OOB retrieves**); advances Mooncake `notify_send_wr_id_` only after `ibv_post_send()` succeeds (**`0` in-flight slot `0` overwrites at the 257th send**) + diagnoses container PID VMM failures; and appends a 16B `treq` tag to NIXL 26B `tctx` notifications (`42B` frame). |
| `flashinfer-mhc-h4096-frost-tvm-ffi-tilelang-pipeline` | [中文](components/flashinfer-mhc-h4096-frost-tvm-ffi-tilelang-pipeline.md) · [English](components/flashinfer-mhc-h4096-frost-tvm-ffi-tilelang-pipeline.en.md) | [`experiments/flashinfer-mhc-h4096-frost-tvm-ffi-tilelang-pipeline/`](../experiments/flashinfer-mhc-h4096-frost-tvm-ffi-tilelang-pipeline/run.py) | `flashinfer-ai/flashinfer` (`21ac136220`, `c0e51ae29b` & `b90521314b`) & `tile-ai/tilelang` (`4b2032a6ce`, `1bafce1a37` & `d9a31ddd1c`): adds `sm_107a` Hidden-4096 fused mHC quantized output variants (`sf_layout="fp8_group128"` `[T, 4096]` + `[T, 32]` FP32 UE8M0 power-of-two scales and `sf_layout="nvfp4"` `[T, 2048]` + `[T, 256]` FP8-E4M3 scales at **`3.5556x` compression**, saving **`1.0 MiB` HBM re-reads at `T=128`** with bitwise-identical base outputs); compiles Frost low-latency MoE into a single TVM-FFI `Pipeline.launch` entry (`"mx-tc-ffi-v1"`, **`2.96x–4.22x` Public API & `5.69x–8.75x` Prepacked Runner speedup on B300 `T=1` SwiGLU: `MXFP4 TC128` `217.82 -> 24.90 us`**); ships StepFun Fused MoE Tile-128 MXFP8 FC1/FC2 (**`1.047x` GB300 & `1.071x` B200 at `T=1024`**); and fixes TileLang transitive pipeline copy worklist `[1, 0]`, thread-domain conflict expansion, and Ascend PTO MAD predicate dedup ($2^{32} \to 1$ node). |
| `sglang-offloader-trtllm-ulysses-qkv-vllm-bad-words` | [中文](components/sglang-offloader-trtllm-ulysses-qkv-vllm-bad-words.md) · [English](components/sglang-offloader-trtllm-ulysses-qkv-vllm-bad-words.en.md) | [`experiments/sglang-offloader-trtllm-ulysses-qkv-vllm-bad-words/`](../experiments/sglang-offloader-trtllm-ulysses-qkv-vllm-bad-words/run.py) | `sgl-project/sglang` (`6d6c9cd4ae`, `494eeecb6c` & `f9129915a3`), `NVIDIA/TensorRT-LLM` (`1586d85375` & `8d1c7e0b26`) & `vllm-project/vllm` (`34b2954482`): scans non-`Parameter` module attributes via `(device, untyped_storage().data_ptr())` in SGLang `OffloaderV2` to automatically pin view-aliased weights (e.g., KDA `qkv_conv1d.weight`) on GPU and bypasses `_VERDICTS` under `torch.compiler.is_compiling()` (**collapsing 40-layer Dynamo recompiles `40 -> 1`**); unscatters `[P, B, Sp, 3, H, D]` directly with `uint4` (8 BF16) loads/stores in TRT-LLM `ulyssesPackedQkvPostUnscatterKernel` (**cutting post-All-to-All HBM traffic by `50.0%`: `301.99 -> 150.99 MB`**) + `checkInt32SumProduct` overflow guards; and aligns vLLM Rust `tokenize_bad_words` (`EmptyBadWord` pre-tokenization rejection, `HashSet` dedup, `max_num_bad_words` cap, and `with_space[0] != without_space[0] && len == len` filter). |
| `te-parallel-ce-argmax-lmcache-2d-copy-nixl-s3-rdma` | [中文](components/te-parallel-ce-argmax-lmcache-2d-copy-nixl-s3-rdma.md) · [English](components/te-parallel-ce-argmax-lmcache-2d-copy-nixl-s3-rdma.en.md) | [`experiments/te-parallel-ce-argmax-lmcache-2d-copy-nixl-s3-rdma/`](../experiments/te-parallel-ce-argmax-lmcache-2d-copy-nixl-s3-rdma/run.py) | `NVIDIA/TransformerEngine` (`39a8b879df` & `76ae4b0981`), `LMCache/LMCache` (`0d441be069`) & `ai-dynamo/nixl` (`99d475be5d` & `14e329032b`): fuses NaN-safe `_fold_block_argmax` (treating `NaN` as maximum with first-NaN/first-tie winner) into TE `parallel_cross_entropy(..., return_argmax=True)` (**saving `125.25 MiB/rank` logits HBM re-reads at `T=4096, V=128256, TP=8` against `16 KiB` all-gather, `8016.0x` ratio**) and zeroes `tensor_base_for_scales` on multi-tensor Grouped MXFP8 (`4096 -> 2048` offset fix); collapses 61-layer × 32-block `BLNHC` transfers via LMCache `uniform_layer_pitch` `cudaMemcpy3DBatchAsync` (**`1,952 -> 32` descriptors, `61.0x` reduction**); and splits objects $> 4\text{ GiB} - 64\text{ KiB}$ (`4,294,901,760 B` `cuObject` token limit) into S3 Multipart RDMA transfers in NIXL (**`9 GiB -> 3` parts, `64 GiB -> 17` parts**) + GPUNetIO peer-VRAM two-SGE WRITE pairing (`64 -> 32` WQEs). |
| `flashinfer-kcp-carry-qsa-nvfp4-mhc-tilelang` | [中文](components/flashinfer-kcp-carry-qsa-nvfp4-mhc-tilelang.md) · [English](components/flashinfer-kcp-carry-qsa-nvfp4-mhc-tilelang.en.md) | [`experiments/flashinfer-kcp-carry-qsa-nvfp4-mhc-tilelang/`](../experiments/flashinfer-kcp-carry-qsa-nvfp4-mhc-tilelang/run.py) | `flashinfer-ai/flashinfer` (`ddc9524d9f`, `34d28b3232` & `85e886c875`) & `tile-ai/tilelang` (`cb26ee0558` & `2573d475cb`): retains `KcpSaved.carry` under `boundary_mode="saved"` (`12.0–80.0 MiB/rank` at $H=64$ when $P>1$, `0 B` when $P=1$) to bypass backward K1 re-walk and carry merge (**`0.9950x–1.0387x` B3 speedup across 144 GB300 rows, `1.0285x` s1 / `1.0284x` s2 geomean on 8 sampled high-CP rows, `0` rows `>2%` slower**); loads K before V with `CacheHint::evict_first` and 1-instruction `prmt.b32` (`0x5140`) in Cake QSA NVFP4 decode (**`1.127x` peak at `rows=32` on H100 `36.42 -> 32.32 us` NHD & `38.83 -> 34.45 us` HND, `1.0852x` mid-batch geomean**); adds SM107 residual `evict_first` and `_claim_launch_epochs` grid-sharing guards in MegaMHC; and rejects `step != 1` in TileLang `VectorizeLoop` while fixing signed `int64` `AtomicMax(-5, 3) == 3` and `AtomicStore` casts. |
| `sglang-marlin-moe-trtllm-kda-swiglu-vllm` | [中文](components/sglang-marlin-moe-trtllm-kda-swiglu-vllm.md) · [English](components/sglang-marlin-moe-trtllm-kda-swiglu-vllm.en.md) | [`experiments/sglang-marlin-moe-trtllm-kda-swiglu-vllm/`](../experiments/sglang-marlin-moe-trtllm-kda-swiglu-vllm/run.py) | `sgl-project/sglang` (`8b9b992e41` & `16aa816f1b`), `NVIDIA/TensorRT-LLM` (`90d4dcbb8f` & `222676cad0`) & `vllm-project/vllm` (`d561af5dfc` & `37cf712552`): maps negative/OOB expert IDs via unsigned `sentinel_expert_bucket` to bucket 0 and publishes `int2(num_tokens_past_padded, inactive_prefix_blocks)` in Marlin WNA16 MoE (**skipping `6/16` `37.5%` inactive prefix blocks via single `int2` load**) while resetting `heartbeat_failures.pop(bootstrap_addr, None)` on disaggregated prefill disconnect; fuses SM100 QuACK BF16 gate/up GEMM with SwiGLU epilogue in TensorRT-LLM (**cutting MiniMax-H3 activation HBM traffic `560.0 -> 112.0 MiB` `-80.0%`, saving `448.0 MiB/layer`**) and integrates FlashInfer KDA `bt16` prefill (**`-5,706` lines**); and guards vLLM Mamba prompt-tail re-caching (`block_hash_num_tokens == num_tokens`, **`3 -> 1` step `3.0x` sibling admission**) while aligning V2 speculative draft RoPE caches via `_CACHE_ONLY_ROPE_TYPES`. |
| `megatron-mfsdp-mxfp8-te-permute-mooncake-nixl` | [中文](components/megatron-mfsdp-mxfp8-te-permute-mooncake-nixl.md) · [English](components/megatron-mfsdp-mxfp8-te-permute-mooncake-nixl.en.md) | [`experiments/megatron-mfsdp-mxfp8-te-permute-mooncake-nixl/`](../experiments/megatron-mfsdp-mxfp8-te-permute-mooncake-nixl/run.py) | `NVIDIA/Megatron-LM` (`18bc49d2f6` & `da2c0ec614`), `NVIDIA/TransformerEngine` (`c9c9225546` & `2199b71664`), `kvcache-ai/Mooncake` (`dd1217b6c4` & `c81f4ad486`), `ai-dynamo/nixl` (`a9f1f7a76f`) & `LMCache/LMCache` (`ff9185d419`): unshards only rowwise in `Phase.FORWARD` and columnwise in `Phase.BACKWARD` under Megatron MFSDPv2 MXFP8 (**cutting per-step All-Gather traffic by `50.0%`: `303.1875 -> 151.5938 MiB`**) and snapshots all 5 mutable fields in `RecomputeSegment` (`mtp_input_mask` preserved); clamps TE Triton mask-map permute `row_id >= num_out_tokens` to `-1` (**blocking `17` over-capacity row writes**) and propagates `record_stream` across all 7 `GroupedTensor` buffers; reads the clock once per `ibv_post_send` chain in Mooncake RDMA (**cutting 256-WR lock hold `6.3 -> 1.9 us` `3.3158x`**) + shard-free DFS eviction & `DeferDeletion`; aligns NIXL `sharedRingBuffer` to `256B` (**`-99.9%` cross-core acquire loads**); and cuts LMCache lookup-to-query latency **`5.40 -> 0.80 ms` (`6.75x`)**. |
| `flashinfer-dsa-sink-dense-gemm-kda-batch-tilelang` | [中文](components/flashinfer-dsa-sink-dense-gemm-kda-batch-tilelang.md) · [English](components/flashinfer-dsa-sink-dense-gemm-kda-batch-tilelang.en.md) | [`experiments/flashinfer-dsa-sink-dense-gemm-kda-batch-tilelang/`](../experiments/flashinfer-dsa-sink-dense-gemm-kda-batch-tilelang/run.py) | `flashinfer-ai/flashinfer` (`2202dd75c1`, `2706a076ac` & `680ca14fa2`) & `tile-ai/tilelang` (`1c1ca63876`, `59c97a8966` & `34cd033ab2`): adds learnable per-head `attention_sink` to 64-head DSA (`m_init = sink[h] * log2(e)`, `dsink_row = -exp2(sink[h]*log2(e) - lse) * delta`) and widens 12 `bwd_pre` kernels from `32` to `128` threads (**`1.0504x` step speedup on GB300 `glm53_b` `32.899 -> 31.321 ms`, `1.0238x` on R200 `doc_4096` `36.701 -> 35.847 ms`**); refactors Cake Dense GEMM into 10 family subpackages + `_router.py` (**removing `31,148` net lines `-69.98%` while preserving `1.0008x` B200 and `1.0005x` GB300 geomean parity**); adds `[B, T, H, D]` fixed-batch `cake_chunk_kda` (`_FixedBatchCuSeqlensCache`) + `chunk_kda_bwd` workspace pass-through; and adds TileLang Blackwell `2x2` TMEM layout guards, CP `cuStreamWaitValue64` sync, and liveness borrow verification. |
| `sglang-moe-lora-gguf-trtllm-spec-nvfp4-vllm-rope` | [中文](components/sglang-moe-lora-gguf-trtllm-spec-nvfp4-vllm-rope.md) · [English](components/sglang-moe-lora-gguf-trtllm-spec-nvfp4-vllm-rope.en.md) | [`experiments/sglang-moe-lora-gguf-trtllm-spec-nvfp4-vllm-rope/`](../experiments/sglang-moe-lora-gguf-trtllm-spec-nvfp4-vllm-rope/run.py) | `sgl-project/sglang` (`2dfae94da7` & `db1e2e2073`), `NVIDIA/TensorRT-LLM` (`3092cf2116` & `fb7461d525`) & `vllm-project/vllm` (`97131637e9` & `bf77355849`): replaces `fused_moe_lora_shrink` `split_k` atomic partials with an in-kernel `BLOCK_K` loop and forces `use_fused_gate=False` on `GGUFUninitializedParameter`; scales TRT-LLM speculative `target_logits /= max(T, 1e-5)` before rejection sampling (**`0.0` TVD vs `0.2156`, restoring acceptance `54.82% -> 81.33%` `+26.51` pts at `T=0.5`**) and searches per-block NVFP4 `E4M3` weight scales over `[0.75, 1.00]` (**`-31.11%` W4A4 MSE**); and eliminates vLLM incremental RoPE `repeat_interleave(2, dim=-1)` via 4D broadcast (**saving `2.0 MiB` scratch/layer**) while passing post-route `num_valid_tokens` to FlashInfer MoE (**cutting `EP=8` scheduled rows `4,096 -> 640`, `-84.38%`**). |
| `torchtitan-mla-rope-swiglu-te-mxfp8-mooncake-nixl` | [中文](components/torchtitan-mla-rope-swiglu-te-mxfp8-mooncake-nixl.md) · [English](components/torchtitan-mla-rope-swiglu-te-mxfp8-mooncake-nixl.en.md) | [`experiments/torchtitan-mla-rope-swiglu-te-mxfp8-mooncake-nixl/`](../experiments/torchtitan-mla-rope-swiglu-te-mxfp8-mooncake-nixl/run.py) | `pytorch/torchtitan` (`2fddc6b7a0`, `21db340cba` & `eb48bab350`), `NVIDIA/TransformerEngine` (`4e8a71b902`), `kvcache-ai/Mooncake` (`9049704a2c`) & `ai-dynamo/nixl` (`c2f4580b18`): compiles DeepSeek-V3/V4.1 MLA `apply_rotary_emb` and `swiglu` with FP32 `RMSNorm` reduction (**cutting per-layer HBM traffic by `696.0 MiB` `-52.27%`/`-60.00%` and raising GB300 671B-A37B throughput `4,120 -> 4,785 tok/s/GPU` `+16.14%`**) and fixes `q_a_proj == None` state-dict mapping; rebinds TE `GroupedLinear` `SWIZZLED_128x4` scale views before grouped GEMMs (**`0.0` vs `1.42e-1` L2 grad error**); adds Mooncake `EvictUnbucketedKeys()` (**`100%` orphan reclaim, `320.0 MiB` freed on 10K keys**); and falls back to `cuDevicePrimaryCtxRetain` for `cuMemCreate` VMM buffers in NIXL Libfabric (**`0% -> 100%` registration**). |

| `flashinfer-vsa-sm90-sage-fp8-kda-kcp-dsa-holefree-conv1d-gdp` | [中文](components/flashinfer-vsa-sm90-sage-fp8-kda-kcp-dsa-holefree-conv1d-gdp.md) · [English](components/flashinfer-vsa-sm90-sage-fp8-kda-kcp-dsa-holefree-conv1d-gdp.en.md) | [`experiments/flashinfer-vsa-sm90-sage-fp8-kda-kcp-dsa-holefree-conv1d-gdp/`](../experiments/flashinfer-vsa-sm90-sage-fp8-kda-kcp-dsa-holefree-conv1d-gdp/run.py) | `flashinfer-ai/flashinfer` (`8ec9464ce8`, `baddd518c5`, `fb23d9702c`, `462a48a4cd`, `69124ac26b`, `8e8342f909` & `562917540d`): expands Hopper `sm_90a` `cake_vsa_sm90` to **40 stages** with Sage-FP8 (**`1.1632x` geomean on H100 across 137 shapes, `1.6968x` peak**); adds KDA KCP `wave_fill1_p1tie` LPT `P=1` tie-breaking, drops `256 * T_r * H` bytes (`256 MiB` per rank at `T_r=16384, H=64`, **`1.464x–2.794x` over FLA on GB300**), and adds `KdaArena(lean=True, tail_passes=4)` (**lowering the backward workspace floor from `4,488` to `2,820 B/token-head`, `-37.17%`**); compacts `-1` holes in 64-head DSA on device (**`2.247x` on GB300**); and adds 6 Causal Conv1d+SiLU routes (**up to `6.649x` on GB300**). |
| `sglang-hicache-mla-dcp-diffusion-lora-trtllm-swa-rubin-vllm-mxfp4` | [中文](components/sglang-hicache-mla-dcp-diffusion-lora-trtllm-swa-rubin-vllm-mxfp4.md) · [English](components/sglang-hicache-mla-dcp-diffusion-lora-trtllm-swa-rubin-vllm-mxfp4.en.md) | [`experiments/sglang-hicache-mla-dcp-diffusion-lora-trtllm-swa-rubin-vllm-mxfp4/`](../experiments/sglang-hicache-mla-dcp-diffusion-lora-trtllm-swa-rubin-vllm-mxfp4/run.py) | `sgl-project/sglang` (`c677231c1d`, `02b983603d` & `0e34962884`), `NVIDIA/TensorRT-LLM` (`ae1aa44864`, `b0af321b0c`, `854ccee8a9`, `448321fb98` & `d7ec5162ac`) & `vllm-project/vllm` (`97b6a67d94`, `7b1d07440d`, `4a5ee8fc77` & `933f7962ae`): enables SGLang HiCache L3 storage with MLA Decode Context Parallelism (**`2x` write deduplication on `TP4/DCP2` and `TRAILING_PAGES` bitmap `ReduceOp.MIN`**) and guards `FUSED_LINEAR_GELU` when dynamic LoRA is active; adds TensorRT-LLM SWA branch snapshots (`point = (divergence // block) * block`), Low-M GEMM capture safety, Rubin `SM107` support, `DecoderLoraOwner`, and Context MLA separate-V stride; and fixes vLLM Humming MXFP4 overrides and Rust 6-tuple `LoRARequest` / `MooncakeTransferStats` wire parity. |
| `megatron-gdp-prefill-a2a-recompute-te-mask-titan-rl-lmcache-mooncake-nixl-dynamo` | [中文](components/megatron-gdp-prefill-a2a-recompute-te-mask-titan-rl-lmcache-mooncake-nixl-dynamo.md) · [English](components/megatron-gdp-prefill-a2a-recompute-te-mask-titan-rl-lmcache-mooncake-nixl-dynamo.en.md) | [`experiments/megatron-gdp-prefill-a2a-recompute-te-mask-titan-rl-lmcache-mooncake-nixl-dynamo/`](../experiments/megatron-gdp-prefill-a2a-recompute-te-mask-titan-rl-lmcache-mooncake-nixl-dynamo/run.py) | `NVIDIA/Megatron-LM` (`fe0e608ca4` & `da2c0ec614`), `NVIDIA/TransformerEngine` (`c9c9225546` & `9ea05cf2e8`), `pytorch/torchtitan` (`43d1bb9801` & `094c884685`), `LMCache/LMCache` (`ff9185d419`, `a81b4e5aea` & `a0ff470128`), `kvcache-ai/Mooncake` (`dd1217b6c4`, `dae22d475e` & `b66bb2b5f6`), `ai-dynamo/nixl` (`f3673f6be3` & `70badd6aad`) & `ai-dynamo/dynamo` (`3acf248073`, `30e972303a`, `c178cbae9b` & `a246c3f227`): fuses ~14 GDP prefill prepare ops into 1 Triton launch and adds `RecomputeSegment` (`5` mutable fields snapshotted + `.detach()`); clamps over-capacity TE routing rows to `-1` and caches FP8 weights under `torch.compile`; reconfigures TorchTitan Muse Spark 816B RL on GB300 from `256` to **`128` GPUs (`8x` smaller expert FSDP group, `28%` MFU)**; cuts Mooncake 256-WR RDMA post chains from **`6.3` to `1.9 us` (`3.32x`)**; and shrinks Dynamo lower-tier router edge slots from **`113` to `33 B` (`3.42x`)**. |
| `flashinfer-cake-kda-tf32-fp8-gemm-stepfun-moe-minimax-h3-cudnn-hnd` | [中文](components/flashinfer-cake-kda-tf32-fp8-gemm-stepfun-moe-minimax-h3-cudnn-hnd.md) · [English](components/flashinfer-cake-kda-tf32-fp8-gemm-stepfun-moe-minimax-h3-cudnn-hnd.en.md) | [`experiments/flashinfer-cake-kda-tf32-fp8-gemm-stepfun-moe-minimax-h3-cudnn-hnd/`](../experiments/flashinfer-cake-kda-tf32-fp8-gemm-stepfun-moe-minimax-h3-cudnn-hnd/README.md) | `flashinfer-ai/flashinfer` (`9f7a290987`, `272ed2a6cf`, `e1b052de72`, `5ea501832b` & `013f02e75d`): folds 24 Cake KDA TF32 kernel clones onto 9 canonical bodies via compile definitions and renders 34 host bindings from 1 template (**reducing `csrc/kda/tf32/` from `97,009` to `41,231` lines, `-57.50%`, and saving `9,497` host-binding lines**); consolidates 4 Cake FP8 GEMM families (`60` programs) with a 56-row routing table (**cutting the batch loader from `6,381` to `1,548` lines, `-75.74%`**); seals `111` StepFun SwiGLU fused-MoE programs across `sm_100a` and `sm_103a` (`222` manifest entries, `47` shared bindings); routes all 8 MiniMax H3 QKV quantize+pack variants (`{MXFP8, NVFP4} × P in {1,2,4,8}`) through the in-tree loader at **`1.000x` B200/GB300 geomean parity**; and declares `"HND"` layout on cuDNN paged prefill to match `[pages, heads=8, page_size=16, dim=128]` tensors (**fixing `qo_heads=40` GQA divisibility `40 / 8 = 5` vs broken `40 % 16 = 8`**). |
| `sglang-extend-host-prompt-reuseport-rust-probe-trtllm-v2-vllm-stream` | [中文](components/sglang-extend-host-prompt-reuseport-rust-probe-trtllm-v2-vllm-stream.md) · [English](components/sglang-extend-host-prompt-reuseport-rust-probe-trtllm-v2-vllm-stream.en.md) | [`experiments/sglang-extend-host-prompt-reuseport-rust-probe-trtllm-v2-vllm-stream/`](../experiments/sglang-extend-host-prompt-reuseport-rust-probe-trtllm-v2-vllm-stream/README.md) | `sgl-project/sglang` (`a3d00eff58`, `7e5256d1ba`, `ca72dad953` & `68623cae92`), `NVIDIA/TensorRT-LLM` (`1c7fadcfbe` & `271ed9f22a`) & `vllm-project/vllm` (`6ae1051bd9` & `5ea2c7ff1e`): computes `contains_last_prefill_chunk` on the host in `ForwardBatch.init_new` and propagates it across TBO child batches (`0` GPU-to-CPU syncs); balances Uvicorn tokenizer workers with per-worker `SO_REUSEPORT` listeners (`--enable-http-worker-reuseport`, `uvicorn>=0.51`); answers all health probes skipped by a busy scheduler on the Rust response ring via a single `frame_health_ack()`; gathers Mamba ping-pong indices via `track_position`; removes obsolete TRT-LLM speculative max-draft CUDA Graph capture (`original_max_draft_len`) and unpacks `(mm_data, mm_embeddings)` for Responses API `input_image` parts; and adds vLLM Rust `--exclude-tools-when-tool-choice-none` / `max_tool_calls: Option<usize>` plus a shared `ChatStreamProcessor` between streaming and `online_derenderer`. |
| `te-fusible-deferred-init-dsink-titan-ulysses-lmcache-fs-mooncake-nixl` | [中文](components/te-fusible-deferred-init-dsink-titan-ulysses-lmcache-fs-mooncake-nixl.md) · [English](components/te-fusible-deferred-init-dsink-titan-ulysses-lmcache-fs-mooncake-nixl.en.md) | [`experiments/te-fusible-deferred-init-dsink-titan-ulysses-lmcache-fs-mooncake-nixl/`](../experiments/te-fusible-deferred-init-dsink-titan-ulysses-lmcache-fs-mooncake-nixl/README.md) | `NVIDIA/TransformerEngine` (`53e241c5a1` & `c3081a5f2d`), `pytorch/torchtitan` (`8899cee5bc` & `5e27dc87b8`), `LMCache/LMCache` (`741b613059`, `786010f4d6` & `32f8aa90b7`), `kvcache-ai/Mooncake` (`7ff6bf7f72` & `292e8391b7`) & `ai-dynamo/nixl` (`d4f72ffe6a` & `8882e2d348`): adds `reset_parameters()` deferred init across `te.ops` fusible ops and narrows the pre-cuDNN 9.26 THD learnable `dSink` guard so `head_dim_v in {64, 128, 256}` specialized kernels stay enabled; sets `NCCL_GRAPH_REGISTER=0` for normal Ulysses CP on `NCCL < 2.32.3` and preserves `AddedToken.special=False` delimiters (`<think>`, `<tool_call>`) via `add_tokens`; decouples LMCache `fs_native` read depth with a `32 MiB` default byte budget (`read_max_bytes_in_flight`), supports dim-0-padded KV views in isolated IPC, and adds Ascend Mamba KV group edits; eliminates bilateral Mooncake EFA active handshake deadlocks via `active_setup_mutex_` + `generation_` and adds configurable `2..60s` etcd lease TTLs; and sets `EVLOOP_NO_EXIT_ON_EMPTY` in NIXL Redis while bounding GPUNetIO notification parsing and preserving embedded `\x00` bytes. |
| `flashinfer-sm120-megamoe-spill-trtllm-multiwave-kimi-k3-nvfp4` | [中文](components/flashinfer-sm120-megamoe-spill-trtllm-multiwave-kimi-k3-nvfp4.md) · [English](components/flashinfer-sm120-megamoe-spill-trtllm-multiwave-kimi-k3-nvfp4.en.md) | [`experiments/flashinfer-sm120-megamoe-spill-trtllm-multiwave-kimi-k3-nvfp4/`](../experiments/flashinfer-sm120-megamoe-spill-trtllm-multiwave-kimi-k3-nvfp4/README.md) | `flashinfer-ai/flashinfer` (`cd6c1885a8`, `a3f2525e39`, `3b65505f40` & `edfb3f7f88`) & `NVIDIA/TensorRT-LLM` (`c018848da6`): eliminates CUDA 13.2–13.4 register spills across **1,440 SM120 Cake MegaMoE JIT instances per toolkit (`0/1440` spills across 11 CUDA 13 releases, `<=168` regs, `0 B` stack on proxy/GDAKI)** via `_CCCL_ATOMIC_UNSAFE_AUTOMATIC_STORAGE`, per-GIN modules, `m128` 1-step K unroll, and a 15-bit signed ABI cap `ACTIVE_ROWS_FIELD_MAX = 0x7FFF` (`max_rows <= 32640`); adds opt-in 4-wave GQA decode KV splitting (`FLASHINFER_TRTLLM_GEN_MULTI_WAVE_KV_SPLIT=1`) when single-wave splits `<= 2`, achieving **`1.86x` geomean speedup on B200 64k lognormal decode and `2.02x` peak at `B=38` 288k lognormal (`1566 -> 781 us`)** with exact scratch fallback; ships 133 Kimi-K3 AttnRes round-2 programs (`161` libraries: **`1.0888x` on GB300 `serving_m5_k5`, `1.1034x` on B200 `k_sweep_m1_k6`**) and self-contained NVFP4 attention (`0.9975x` GB300 parity); and replaces `video_np.tobytes()` with `memoryview(video_np.reshape(-1))` in TRT-LLM VisualGen (**saving a `711.91 MiB` host allocation and copy**). |
| `sglang-dsv4-window-sparse-prefill-indexk-cache-radix-offload-vllm-moe` | [中文](components/sglang-dsv4-window-sparse-prefill-indexk-cache-radix-offload-vllm-moe.md) · [English](components/sglang-dsv4-window-sparse-prefill-indexk-cache-radix-offload-vllm-moe.en.md) | [`experiments/sglang-dsv4-window-sparse-prefill-indexk-cache-radix-offload-vllm-moe/`](../experiments/sglang-dsv4-window-sparse-prefill-indexk-cache-radix-offload-vllm-moe/README.md) | `sgl-project/sglang` (`f7d4f06242`, `bc412213c1`, `97945831f9`, `620718c8ea` & `2c7a9e3b50`) & `vllm-project/vllm` (`b0e1f8052a`): precomputes DeepSeek-V4.1 `WindowLayout.history_src` once per batch (**`60x` fewer 2D fancy-index gathers: `60 -> 1`**), caches `low_ratio_k_slots` and `FastIndexPlan` per compression ratio (`r in {4, 8}`) on `DSV4Metadata` (**`24x` fewer index-k gathers across 48 layers: `48 -> 2`**), and restores sparse prefill when `request_window` is active; orders `UnifiedCacheLinker` (`MooncakeDirectLinker`, `UMBPDirectLinker`) async offloads behind both `current_stream` and an `_offload_ready_event` recorded on `forward_stream` (**eliminating `12 us < 25 us` read-before-write KV corruption**); and adds vLLM monolithic MoE `supports_monolithic_kernels` + `prepare_monolithic_bias()` alongside SGLang DSV4.1 `vision_topk` unpacked-ID guards under routing simulation. |
| `megatron-gdn-cudagraph-titan-gradclip-lmcache-prefetch-vmm-mooncake-nixl` | [中文](components/megatron-gdn-cudagraph-titan-gradclip-lmcache-prefetch-vmm-mooncake-nixl.md) · [English](components/megatron-gdn-cudagraph-titan-gradclip-lmcache-prefetch-vmm-mooncake-nixl.en.md) | [`experiments/megatron-gdn-cudagraph-titan-gradclip-lmcache-prefetch-vmm-mooncake-nixl/`](../experiments/megatron-gdn-cudagraph-titan-gradclip-lmcache-prefetch-vmm-mooncake-nixl/README.md) | `NVIDIA/Megatron-LM` (`29e1a7b696`), `pytorch/torchtitan` (`9698a75462` & `a243375253`), `LMCache/LMCache` (`a6accee6d8`, `21aa6ac06c` & `45486917f1`), `kvcache-ai/Mooncake` (`7a05855674`), `ai-dynamo/nixl` (`03d9b74648`) & `ai-dynamo/dynamo` (`5004f3fa80`): models Gated DeltaNet (GDN) prefill CUDA graph static `cu_seqlens` buffers as a single-update GDP (`num_householder=1`, `64` chunk descriptors at `T=4096`); moves gradient clipping into TorchTitan's `OptimizersContainer` as an optimizer-owned `GradientClipper` shared with `TorchFT` and delegates `_LinearFunction.spmd_typecheck` to `einsum` bilinearity (`Partial @ Replicate -> Partial`) for LoRA under TP; replaces LMCache's fixed `max_in_flight=8` with `--l2-prefetch-inflight-watermark=0.5` (**`4.0x` higher `8 MiB` concurrency: `32 vs 8`**), fixes `VmmCudaIPCWrapper` dim-0-padded KV pool byte span (`3584 B` vs `2048 B`), and binds the primary CUDA context in `stream_ops`; enables Mooncake CPU-to-GPU TCP memory selection; adds NIXL 64-bit `xfer_request_id` & `xfer_execution` telemetry; and propagates Dynamo multimodal image token errors as `None` instead of `Some(0)`. |
| `flashinfer-cake-fp4-ar-mnnvl-fp32-sage-fp8-bsa-trtllm-moe-ws-k3-situ` | [中文](components/flashinfer-cake-fp4-ar-mnnvl-fp32-sage-fp8-bsa-trtllm-moe-ws-k3-situ.md) · [English](components/flashinfer-cake-fp4-ar-mnnvl-fp32-sage-fp8-bsa-trtllm-moe-ws-k3-situ.en.md) | [`experiments/flashinfer-cake-fp4-ar-mnnvl-fp32-sage-fp8-bsa-trtllm-moe-ws-k3-situ/`](../experiments/flashinfer-cake-fp4-ar-mnnvl-fp32-sage-fp8-bsa-trtllm-moe-ws-k3-situ/README.md) | `flashinfer-ai/flashinfer` (`067b36956d`, `1ff146212d`, `4e4c41da67`, `15c83f1c36`, `75b30ffdbf` & `e2456a4eea`): ships 37 per-architecture (`sm_100a`/`sm_103a`/`sm_107a`) NVFP4-transport all-reduce + RMSNorm programs (`_rso`, `_rst`, `_qp`, `_sop`, `_pf`) reaching **`1.0718x` geomean on B200 (`1.0942x` peak at `8K x 6144` `NVFP4 -> NVFP4`: `77.36 -> 70.70 us`)**; casts to `float` before squaring in MNNVL one-shot fused allreduce + RMSNorm so FP16 activations with `|x| > 255.94` do not overflow to `inf` (**restoring Qwen3.5-27B FP16 TP=2 RL score from `0.000` to `0.698`**); merges SM100/SM103 Sage-FP8 block-sparse attention into 1 launch (**`1.30x–1.40x` faster on GB300, `1.23x` faster fused quantizers**); adds `trtllm_fused_moe_workspace_size` and caller-owned `workspace_buffer=` (**cutting B300 autotuning peak memory from `3,579.9 MiB` to `896.8 MiB`, `-2,683.1 MiB`**); and folds Kimi-K3 SiTU MoE by **`-17,018` lines (`-22.60%`)** with 100% identical SASS. |
| `sglang-dsv41-mxfp8-comfyui-flux-trtllm-exec-vllm-bert-lora` | [中文](components/sglang-dsv41-mxfp8-comfyui-flux-trtllm-exec-vllm-bert-lora.md) · [English](components/sglang-dsv41-mxfp8-comfyui-flux-trtllm-exec-vllm-bert-lora.en.md) | [`experiments/sglang-dsv41-mxfp8-comfyui-flux-trtllm-exec-vllm-bert-lora/`](../experiments/sglang-dsv41-mxfp8-comfyui-flux-trtllm-exec-vllm-bert-lora/README.md) | `sgl-project/sglang` (`028de2c5d4` & `62b4127668`), `NVIDIA/TensorRT-LLM` (`a0141d0e56` & `00c184e1f8`) & `vllm-project/vllm` (`e8e43c6a09`, `a0a645abda` & `c84edac4cb`): lowers DeepSeek-V4.1 `mxfp8_prefill_autotune_min_tokens` from `4096` to `1` on FlashInfer CuTeDSL (**unlocking `14/14` tuned decode/prefill buckets vs `2/14`**) and deduplicates ComfyUI Flux ControlNet residuals via `_memo[id(value)]` (**`5.7x` lower IPC pickle & `shard_latents_for_sp` work across `57` blocks**) with `_SESSION_SKIP_EXTRA` step isolation; retires **`35` legacy executor `MpiTag` enum entries** and 6 compatibility files while enabling `ENABLE_BOLT_PREMERGE_CONSUME = true` and nightly release BOLT variant publishing; and adds `TokenClassificationHeadWithLoRA` with `AllPool(output_sizes_fn)` slicing (`[[12,3],[8,9],[15,5]]`), shared-GPU NIXL prefill-first barrier, and ROCm EAGLE3 `VLLM_BATCH_INVARIANT=1`. |
