| `nixl-gpunetio-read-coalesce-ucx-cpu-proxy-mooncake-tent-abi-dynamo-planner-torchtitan-te-ep` | [中文](components/nixl-gpunetio-read-coalesce-ucx-cpu-proxy-mooncake-tent-abi-dynamo-planner-torchtitan-te-ep.md) · [English](components/nixl-gpunetio-read-coalesce-ucx-cpu-proxy-mooncake-tent-abi-dynamo-planner-torchtitan-te-ep.en.md) | [`experiments/nixl_gpunetio_read_ucx_proxy_mooncake_tent_dynamo_planner_torchtitan_te/`](../experiments/nixl_gpunetio_read_ucx_proxy_mooncake_tent_dynamo_planner_torchtitan_te/README.md) | `ai-dynamo/nixl` (`5ef18e238e`, `e699ecbb3b` & `cb193c9c1c`), `kvcache-ai/Mooncake` (`b3de891c34` & `938f052ac9`), `ai-dynamo/dynamo` (`2ba6e07f60`), `pytorch/torchtitan` (`ca558e1201` & `6db08e59d2`) & `NVIDIA/TransformerEngine` (`ebfd0e774a`): coalesces contiguous GPUNetIO READs across the 512-entry boundary up to `8 MiB` (**`4,096 -> 2` WQEs, `8 -> 2` ring entries, `+109.92%` throughput `10.97 -> 23.03 GB/s`, `-52.11%` p99 `1,516 -> 726 us` on 2×H20**) and ships UCX multi-threaded CPU Proxy EP (**`82.97 / 80.23 GB/s` dispatch/combine on 16×GB200** at 8ch/8thr); aligns Rust `TentRequest` to 48-byte C ABI (`priority@40`, `transport_hint@44`) and snapshots `TimeKey` before sorting; guards `/engine/planner` with HTTP 409 revision checks and `16 MiB`/`4,096`-step off-loop predictor warmup; activates `ChunkedLossWrapper` `head_has_trainable_params` for frozen-decoder `lm_head` LoRA and replaces TE JAX static EP handle cache with `ncclEpImportHandle`. |
| `flashinfer-cake-mhc-glm53-msa-kmajor-fp4-allreduce-multiwave-bmm-order` | [中文](components/flashinfer-cake-mhc-glm53-msa-kmajor-fp4-allreduce-multiwave-bmm-order.md) · [English](components/flashinfer-cake-mhc-glm53-msa-kmajor-fp4-allreduce-multiwave-bmm-order.en.md) | [`experiments/flashinfer_cake_mhc_msa_kmajor_multiwave_bmm/`](../experiments/flashinfer_cake_mhc_msa_kmajor_multiwave_bmm/README.md) | `flashinfer-ai/flashinfer` (`130dd2429a`, `2ff6d8a5f2`, `3b65505f40`, `c7d1eacfdc`, `a3f2525e39` & `c1bcf70660`): implements 4-stream GLM-5.3-Flash mHC training (`streams=4, hidden=4096`, 19-step Sinkhorn-Knopp doubly stochastic mixing, `256 B/token` saved stats vs `0 B/token` checkpoint replay) on SM100/SM103/SM107, delivering **`15.95x` forward (`1454.7 us` vs `23197.5 us`) and `14.79x` checkpoint (`2823.8 us` vs `41773.9 us`) on GB300 at `T=65536`** (`14.28x` ckpt on R200); adds 3 persistent K-major prefill routes to `cake_blackwell_msa` (**`3.39x` GB300 geomean across `106` shapes**, peak `5.284x` paged prefill) and Round-2 Kimi-K3 AttnRes; overlaps TP2 row-scale reads and retains TP4 pass-1 validated NVFP4 packets in SMEM (**`3,475 -> 16 B/row` L2 re-read, `-99.54%`**); sizes 4-wave KV split `partialStats` by `max(SMs, totalNumCtas) * stepQ` (`9,216` vs `2,368`) and enforces strict weak ordering in BMM default selection (**`1.90x` on NVFP4 FC2: `0.135 -> 0.071 ms`**). |
| `trtllm-minimax-m3-bf16-split-router-flux-fp8-sglang-quark-asm-vllm-moriio` | [中文](components/trtllm-minimax-m3-bf16-split-router-flux-fp8-sglang-quark-asm-vllm-moriio.md) · [English](components/trtllm-minimax-m3-bf16-split-router-flux-fp8-sglang-quark-asm-vllm-moriio.en.md) | [`experiments/trtllm_minimax_m3_router_flux_sglang_quark_vllm_moriio/`](../experiments/trtllm_minimax_m3_router_flux_sglang_quark_vllm_moriio/README.md) | `NVIDIA/TensorRT-LLM` (`522daa8cdf`, `5d6fbb2062` & `5f90e8939d`), `sgl-project/sglang` (`6e98c84c60`, `ed268dd320` & `ca72dad953`) & `vllm-project/vllm` (`db3b0f0fae` & `35d01660ff`): decomposes MiniMax-M3 FP32 router weights into `3` residual BF16 pieces `[E, 3H]` (`24` mantissa bits total), achieving **`0.0` (`< 1e-9`) logit drift vs `1.16e-4` TF32 drift** in a single BF16 tensor-core GEMM, plus SM103 `fusedDiTQKNormRopeQuantFp8Kernel` and custom-mask FP8 KV/out scaling; implements Quark AITER ASM FP4 7D E8M0 scale swizzling (`32x8` bijection, `256`-row `0x7F` padding, MI355X `N<=128` safety guard), DeepSeek-V4.1 vision-biased MoE routing simulation, and Rust response-ring health-probe acks; moves ROCm MoRIIO read completion await into `start_load_kv` before forward with `math.ceil(timeout * 1000)` ms deadline. |
| `dynamo-kv-state-agent-atomic-cursor-1000slot-nixl-multiqp-mooncake-ascend-mmap-torchtitan-mla-cp` | [中文](components/dynamo-kv-state-agent-atomic-cursor-1000slot-nixl-multiqp-mooncake-ascend-mmap-torchtitan-mla-cp.md) · [English](components/dynamo-kv-state-agent-atomic-cursor-1000slot-nixl-multiqp-mooncake-ascend-mmap-torchtitan-mla-cp.en.md) | [`experiments/dynamo_kv_cursor_1000slot_nixl_multiqp_torchtitan_mla_cp/`](../experiments/dynamo_kv_cursor_1000slot_nixl_multiqp_torchtitan_mla_cp/README.md) | `ai-dynamo/dynamo` (`93ff081d4e`, `9906dbe5ee` & `5235cfc93d`), `ai-dynamo/nixl` (`bb71164c4b`), `kvcache-ai/Mooncake` (`4137ac0026` & `7a05855674`) & `pytorch/torchtitan` (`0980e1cb1c` & `9698a75462`): decouples per-chunk `outbound_cursor: AtomicU64` from `ArcSwap<KvStateAgentStatus>` (**`10,002 -> 2` `ArcSwap::store` calls, `-99.98%`**) and pools `1,024` DP slots with 16-hex SHA-256 topics and `--slots-per-socket=8` (**`2,048 -> 256` ZMQ sockets, `8x` reduction**); stripes NIXL GPUNetIO RDMA WRITEs across `1..4` QPs in 1 launch with last-arrival tail posting and rebases Mooncake Ascend A5 `mmap`/`memfd` host segments; all-gathers compressed MLA latents `(kv_c_normed_TL, k_shared_TR)` (**`576` vs `40,960` elems/token, `71.11x` reduction**) and fuses Ulysses CP (`4 -> 2` All-to-Alls). |
| `flashinfer-masked-mxfp8-silu-mul-sm90-megamoe-push-cute-bmm-fp8-scale` | [中文](components/flashinfer-masked-mxfp8-silu-mul-sm90-megamoe-push-cute-bmm-fp8-scale.md) · [English](components/flashinfer-masked-mxfp8-silu-mul-sm90-megamoe-push-cute-bmm-fp8-scale.en.md) | [`experiments/flashinfer_masked_mxfp8_sm90_megamoe_bmm_fp8/`](../experiments/flashinfer_masked_mxfp8_sm90_megamoe_bmm_fp8/README.md) | `flashinfer-ai/flashinfer` (`140f9fc4c5`, `da992ed991`, `bf044ba86e` & `617845d1a3`): adds SM100+ `cvt_fp16_to_mxfp8_expert` & fused `silu_and_mul_mxfp8_experts_quantize` on B200 (`E=32, CAP=1024`), cutting GEMM1 quantize latency **`59.17 -> 5.90 us` (`10.03x` vs `cutile`)** and fused SiLU+Mul+MXFP8 **`91.22 -> 4.88 us` (`18.69x` at 16 valid rows/expert)**; fixes SM90 FP4 MegaMoE inactive-warp `fetch_expert_recv_count()` workspace cleanup (**`112 -> 0` dirty blocks** at 128 hot-expert tokens), enables H200 EP8 push dispatch (`M<=32` and `M=33..64` rotated pool, `~10 us/call`), and enforces `6 * scale <= 448` via power-of-two global scales; fuses `a_scale[0] * b_scale[0]` into `bmm_fp8` epilogue and gates `mm_mxfp8` warp split-K on `N // 16 <= SM_count` (`N <= 2368`), eliminating **`63/150` B300 and `43/120` B200 PDL regressions**. |
| `sglang-mooncake-failed-session-probe-glm52-side-stream-trtllm-glm53-sparse-sm107-mxfp8` | [中文](components/sglang-mooncake-failed-session-probe-glm52-side-stream-trtllm-glm53-sparse-sm107-mxfp8.md) · [English](components/sglang-mooncake-failed-session-probe-glm52-side-stream-trtllm-glm53-sparse-sm107-mxfp8.en.md) | [`experiments/sglang_mooncake_probe_glm52_trtllm_glm53_sparse/`](../experiments/sglang_mooncake_probe_glm52_trtllm_glm53_sparse/README.md) | `sgl-project/sglang` (`be65391abd`, `2e7c9d851c`, `f7b8c6fe40` & `da7e3f1c12`) & `NVIDIA/TensorRT-LLM` (`ef913d39b3` & `4636b6311f`): default-enables Mooncake failed-session probe with exponential backoff (`30s -> 300s`) and `600s` idle pruning (**`2880 -> 4` probes/24h, `-99.86%`**) + per-GPU NIXL UCX `device_list` binding; records GLM-5.2 DSA side-stream work right before `wait_stream` for single-stream CUDA-graph replay and supports 1-to-many packed draft layers in `UMBPDirectLinker`; accelerates GLM-5.3-Flash `glm_kpool` via single-launch `_append_paged_state_kernel` (**`6 -> 1` launches**), 2D draft-verify same-pool skip (**`8 -> 3` updates**), and native TRTLLM-GEN FP8 decode up to `128` heads + Rubin `SM107` MXFP8 MoE. |
| `megatron-mfsdp-v2-execution-order-graph-grad-reuse-mooncake-shard-cursor-xpu-dmabuf` | [中文](components/megatron-mfsdp-v2-execution-order-graph-grad-reuse-mooncake-shard-cursor-xpu-dmabuf.md) · [English](components/megatron-mfsdp-v2-execution-order-graph-grad-reuse-mooncake-shard-cursor-xpu-dmabuf.en.md) | [`experiments/megatron_mfsdp_v2_exec_order_grad_reuse_mooncake_cursor/`](../experiments/megatron_mfsdp_v2_exec_order_grad_reuse_mooncake_cursor/README.md) | `NVIDIA/Megatron-LM` (`60547a3f41`, `66a6d3f716` & `db3a6cd8a0`), `NVIDIA/TransformerEngine` (`5eeb62a2fc`) & `kvcache-ai/Mooncake` (`0ed2fdfc68`, `e3e9d70eea` & `042ab8a18b`): replaces `IndexedOrder` with `ExecutionOrder` in MFSDP v2 to validate repeated module calls and look ahead without advancing the cursor, shares the forward/backward CUDA-graph pool in `FullIterationGradCopy` (**saving `4.0 GB` VRAM** on a 4 GB FP32 grad shard setup), and fixes EP MoE DTensor checkpointing; caches `self._op` in TE `LinearAttentionBase.__init__` (**`1 -> 0` Dynamo graph breaks**); replaces Mooncake `tenant_object_index_` with shard-backed `TenantCursor` without epoch-bumping `ref` re-locks and restores exact `CUcontext` in `CudaPlatform::copy()`. |
| `flashinfer-cake-gdp-nemotron4-nvfp4-4over6-slot-append-causal-conv1d-bwd` | [中文](components/flashinfer-cake-gdp-nemotron4-nvfp4-4over6-slot-append-causal-conv1d-bwd.md) · [English](components/flashinfer-cake-gdp-nemotron4-nvfp4-4over6-slot-append-causal-conv1d-bwd.en.md) | [`experiments/flashinfer_cake_gdp_nvfp4_4over6_conv1d_bwd/`](../experiments/flashinfer_cake_gdp_nvfp4_4over6_conv1d_bwd/README.md) | `flashinfer-ai/flashinfer` (`fc1790f4ec`, `52fbbd090c`, `3821440929` & `7c866e85d3`): ships `cake_gated_delta_product_mtp` for Nemotron 4 (`K=128, V=64, n in {1..4}`) on `sm_100a`/`sm_103a` with `splitv8_tile8` (`T=1 & B*HV<=160`, 8 CTAs/head) and `lanerow` (`l8r4`/`l8r2`/`l4r4`, 1 CTA/head), winning **`48/48` shapes on GB200 (`1.01x–3.99x`, peak `301.4 -> 75.5 us`)** and **`47/48` on GB300 (`0.98x–3.95x`, peak `296.6 -> 75.1 us`)**; adds `nvfp4_4over6` block scale selection (`-99.65%` sub-peak clustered block MSE, `51` default kernels SASS-identical) and removes `.cpu()` scale syncs (**`499.4 -> 451.7 ms`, `-47.7 ms` / `-9.55%`** on SM80 20-layer Qwen3.6-35B-A3B NVFP4 eager prefill); updates `cake_causal_conv1d_train` backward channel routing (**`1.0164x` on GB300 `C=512`, `1.0331x` across 11 routed shapes on Rubin R200**). |
| `trtllm-dkg-cute-dsa-mqa-logits-kv-transfer-contract-vllm-shm-watchdog` | [中文](components/trtllm-dkg-cute-dsa-mqa-logits-kv-transfer-contract-vllm-shm-watchdog.md) · [English](components/trtllm-dkg-cute-dsa-mqa-logits-kv-transfer-contract-vllm-shm-watchdog.en.md) | [`experiments/trtllm_dkg_dsa_mqa_kv_contract_vllm_watchdog/`](../experiments/trtllm_dkg_dsa_mqa_kv_contract_vllm_watchdog/README.md) | `NVIDIA/TensorRT-LLM` (`033f2ea020`, `a9000fe10e` & `16d26df5a7`) & `vllm-project/vllm` (`1c87991978`): default-enables `use_cute_dsl_paged_mqa_logits=True` on `sm_100f` with `_pick_dsl_split` + `_atom_ctx_offsets` in-kernel uneven splitting for prime `next_n` (`next_n=5 -> 2` atoms `[2,0]` **`-60.0%` KV reads**; `next_n=7 -> 3` atoms `[4,2,0]` **`-57.14%` KV reads, `1.04x–2.60x` on GB300** at `>=24` waves); defines the formal disaggregated KV `Backend` contract (`SlotRegion`, `View`, `Outcome`, `Attempt.quiesced()` memory-reuse gate) + `0.1s` MPI teardown `SHUTDOWN_ACK`; adds vLLM `ThreadedWatchDog` (`timeout=300s`, `0` false dumps during `shm_broadcast` idle waits via `wd.disable()`, one-shot stack dump per stall). |
| `megatron-hybrid-dsa-gqa-gdn-cudagraph-mooncake-store-reshard-tenant-quota-nixl` | [中文](components/megatron-hybrid-dsa-gqa-gdn-cudagraph-mooncake-store-reshard-tenant-quota-nixl.md) · [English](components/megatron-hybrid-dsa-gqa-gdn-cudagraph-mooncake-store-reshard-tenant-quota-nixl.en.md) | [`experiments/megatron_dsa_gqa_gdn_mooncake_store_reshard_quota/`](../experiments/megatron_dsa_gqa_gdn_mooncake_store_reshard_quota/README.md) | `NVIDIA/Megatron-LM` (`d14b699755` & `29e1a7b696`), `kvcache-ai/Mooncake` (`e2d6e8e0f2` & `44c4d9cf4a`) & `ai-dynamo/nixl` (`03d9b74648`): adds hybrid `DSGroupedSelfAttention` + unscaled `raw_values` loss logging and routes GDN varlen prefill through `num_householder=1` `chunk_gated_delta_product_varlen` with fixed-capacity chunk descriptors for CUDA Graph replay; plans Mooncake Store KV reuse with `replica_ordinal==0` upload writer deduplication (**`-50.0%` writers** at 2x replication) + `HeadIntervalIndex.cover` target replica loads and two-word `Held/Carried` upsert quota accounting with slab watermark eviction; adds non-zero 64-bit `nixl.xfer_request_id` and `nixl.xfer_execution` telemetry counters. |
| `flashinfer-rubin-nvfp4-mla-decode-mnnvl-collective-handle` | [中文](components/flashinfer-rubin-nvfp4-mla-decode-mnnvl-collective-handle.md) · [English](components/flashinfer-rubin-nvfp4-mla-decode-mnnvl-collective-handle.en.md) | [`experiments/flashinfer_rubin_nvfp4_mla_mnnvl_handle/run.py`](../experiments/flashinfer_rubin_nvfp4_mla_mnnvl_handle/run.py) | `flashinfer-ai/flashinfer` (`adca7b87d2` & `a48bf8af3c`): `CakeMlaNvfp4PagedDecode` on NVIDIA Rubin (`sm_107a`) stores **`352 B/token`** (`256 B` E2M1 + `32 B` E4M3 scales + `64 B` E4M3 RoPE, **`-69.44%` vs BF16**), quantizes queries online (`356 B/row`), and schedules mixed 256-row 4-CTA units + 128-row 2-CTA pairs via `mla2_plan`, achieving **`1.044x` overall / `1.104x` `Q=4` geomean and `1.248x` peak (`1300.8 vs 1623.8 us`)** on pre-production R200 (`author-benchmark`); `choose_mnnvl_handle_type` & `allocate_mnnvl_handles` coordinate stateless collective handle selection, attempting `FABRIC` even when NVML `clusterUuid` is all-zero and collectively falling back to `POSIX_FD` with `cft_enabled=False` when IMEX channels are absent. |
| `sglang-pd-dp-role-switch-vllm-dsv41-replay-rows` | [中文](components/sglang-pd-dp-role-switch-vllm-dsv41-replay-rows.md) · [English](components/sglang-pd-dp-role-switch-vllm-dsv41-replay-rows.en.md) | [`experiments/sglang_pd_dp_role_switch_vllm_dsv41_replay/run.py`](../experiments/sglang_pd_dp_role_switch_vllm_dsv41_replay/run.py) | `sgl-project/sglang` (`af49ada16d`) & `vllm-project/vllm` (`85752a43c3`): unblocks live PD role switching under `--dp-size > 1` and `--enable-dp-attention` using a two-phase `prepare_only=True` preflight before mutating scheduler role or tearing down KV transfer queues (**`0` split-brain / `0` torn-down queues** when Rank 3 fails precheck vs `3 Decode + 1 Prefill` under single-phase commit); includes `req.prompt_logprob_token_ids is not None` in `_prepare_deepseek_v41_replay_for_current_pass` so DeepSeek-V4.1 target-token scoring under `dcp_size=4` retains all **`16 / 16` prompt rows** instead of slicing to `1` row. |
| `megatron-lsmuon-ns-stream-te-align-mooncake-acc-quota-nixl` | [中文](components/megatron-lsmuon-ns-stream-te-align-mooncake-acc-quota-nixl.md) · [English](components/megatron-lsmuon-ns-stream-te-align-mooncake-acc-quota-nixl.en.md) | [`experiments/megatron_lsmuon_stream_mooncake_acc_quota_nixl/run.py`](../experiments/megatron_lsmuon_stream_mooncake_acc_quota_nixl/run.py) | `NVIDIA/Megatron-LM` (`6c956376e3` & `45b68f914c`), `kvcache-ai/Mooncake` (`3b08b77168` & `44c4d9cf4a`) & `ai-dynamo/nixl` (`f8248873bd`): `LayerShardedMuon._iter_ns` yields `ns_batch_size=8` generator batches to cut 128-layer `[2048,2048]` peak transient buffer **`4096 -> 256 MiB` (`16.0x` reduction)** while TE `GroupedLinear` enforces `alignment=256`; Mooncake ACC dynamically bounds RDMA windows (`4..64 MiB`, `2 ms` drain target, RNR backoff, empty-flight oversize admit) and `TenantQuotaPolicy` tracks `Held + Carried` slots across overwrite (`owed=900 -> 500 B`); NIXL records `nixl::xfer.complete` immediately on synchronous `NIXL_SUCCESS` (**`50 / 50` trace parity**). |
| `flashinfer-rubin-nvfp4-mla-decode-stepfun-pair-raster-moe-vllm-dsv41-replay` | [中文](components/flashinfer-rubin-nvfp4-mla-decode-stepfun-pair-raster-moe-vllm-dsv41-replay.md) · [English](components/flashinfer-rubin-nvfp4-mla-decode-stepfun-pair-raster-moe-vllm-dsv41-replay.en.md) | [`experiments/flashinfer_rubin_nvfp4_mla_stepfun_moe_vllm_dsv41/`](../experiments/flashinfer_rubin_nvfp4_mla_stepfun_moe_vllm_dsv41/README.md) | `flashinfer-ai/flashinfer` (`adca7b87d2` & `fd4de5acce`) & `vllm-project/vllm` (`85752a43c3`): adds Rubin (`sm_107a` / R200 208 SMs) Cake dense NVFP4 MLA paged decode over a **`352 B/token`** KV cache and **`356 B/row`** quantized query buffer with mixed 4-CTA (`50`) and 2-CTA (`4`) clusters (**`1.0436x` overall geomean, `1.1039x` Q4 geomean, `1.2483x` peak: `1623.8 -> 1300.8 us`** vs TokenSpeed MLA 2.0.3); regenerates `111` StepFun SwiGLU MoE kernels/arch on `sm_100a`/`sm_103a` with pair-rasterized BF16 FC1 (`2.0x` L2 weight reuse) and 2-CTA `nvfp4_bf16tok` FC2 (**`1.0323x` on B200, `1.0245x` on GB300** at `T=1024`); fixes DeepSeek-V4.1 `prompt_logprob_token_ids` replay to preserve all **`300` rows** vs `WINDOW=64` truncation. |
| `sglang-pd-dp-attention-role-switch-trtllm-dp-kv-event-flush-mooncake-zerocopy` | [中文](components/sglang-pd-dp-attention-role-switch-trtllm-dp-kv-event-flush-mooncake-zerocopy.md) · [English](components/sglang-pd-dp-attention-role-switch-trtllm-dp-kv-event-flush-mooncake-zerocopy.en.md) | [`experiments/sglang_pd_dp_role_switch_trtllm_adp_kv_mooncake_zc/`](../experiments/sglang_pd_dp_role_switch_trtllm_adp_kv_mooncake_zc/README.md) | `sgl-project/sglang` (`af49ada16d`) & `NVIDIA/TensorRT-LLM` (`ec782566f5` & `69e06b8afb`): enables PD role switching under `dp_size > 1` and `--enable-dp-attention` via a two-phase `prepare_only=True` preflight (**`0` torn-down ranks** on partial OOM vs `3/4` split-brain); moves `_add_kv_cache_events()` outside rank-divergent `_process_previous_batch()` and runs rebalance collectives on `absent` ADP ranks (**`3/3` transcripts deadlock-free**); enables Mooncake Store zero-copy (`stage_through_host=False`) by cutting V2 VMM pools and straddling pages at `cuMemCreate` boundaries (`[2 MiB, 2 MiB]`), saving **`1,024 MiB` pinned host DRAM** per 8-GPU node. |
| `megatron-layersharded-muon-stream-te-cublasmp-nvfp4-mooncake-rdma-cc-nixl-xfer` | [中文](components/megatron-layersharded-muon-stream-te-cublasmp-nvfp4-mooncake-rdma-cc-nixl-xfer.md) · [English](components/megatron-layersharded-muon-stream-te-cublasmp-nvfp4-mooncake-rdma-cc-nixl-xfer.en.md) | [`experiments/megatron_muon_stream_te_cublasmp_nvfp4_mooncake_cc_nixl/`](../experiments/megatron_muon_stream_te_cublasmp_nvfp4_mooncake_cc_nixl/README.md) | `NVIDIA/Megatron-LM` (`6c956376e3`), `NVIDIA/TransformerEngine` (`6b5cd7e2e4`), `kvcache-ai/Mooncake` (`3b08b77168`) & `ai-dynamo/nixl` (`f8248873bd`): `LayerShardedMuon._iter_ns` streams local batched Newton-Schulz with in-place scaling on single-rank MoE domains, cutting NS launches **`17 -> 5` (`3.4x`)** and peak transient FP32 memory **`1,088.0 -> 192.0 MiB` (`5.6967x`)**; TE `cuBLASMp >= 0.11.0` adds NVFP4 (`VEC16_UE4M3`) overlap via **`12 B`** device pointer-mode scalars `{alpha, 0, 1}`, `(N/tp)%128!=0` fallback, and single dual-usage `grad_output` quantization; Mooncake adds `[4, 64] MiB` AIMD RDMA congestion control and NIXL emits **`50/50`** `nixl::xfer.complete` UCX marks. |
| `flashinfer-cake-grouped-mxfp8-quantize-vllm-dsv4-eplb-redundant-slots` | [中文](components/flashinfer-cake-grouped-mxfp8-quantize-vllm-dsv4-eplb-redundant-slots.md) · [English](components/flashinfer-cake-grouped-mxfp8-quantize-vllm-dsv4-eplb-redundant-slots.en.md) | [`experiments/flashinfer_cake_grouped_mxfp8_vllm_dsv4_eplb_kimi/`](../experiments/flashinfer_cake_grouped_mxfp8_vllm_dsv4_eplb_kimi/README.md) | `flashinfer-ai/flashinfer` (`d8ddf1a367`) & `vllm-project/vllm` (`9d4192aaa1` & `56e67b46a3`): routes `cake_grouped_mxfp8_quantize` through the in-tree `CakeProgram` loader (`d8aa920df5a6252453bd` BF16, `f84d4eab2905894ac4c2` FP16 via `a.view(torch.bfloat16)`) on `sm_100a` and `sm_103a` with in-kernel 128-column padding, `[B, pm_tiles, pk_tiles, 32, 4, 4]` E8M0 scale layout, and **`3.808/4.000 us` (B200) and `3.776/3.648 us` (GB300)** latency (`0.983x–1.000x`); forwards `num_redundant_experts` in DeepSeek-V4 `get_expert_mapping()` and verifies byte-sum parity via `check_eplb_replica_slots` (**`8 -> 0` unloaded redundant slots**); configures Kimi-K3 across four 4-GPU GB200 trays as **`TP8 x PP2 + DCP8 (a2a)`** (`-50%` PP stages) with `TRTLLM_RAGGED` MLA prefill and `K=4` DSpark. |
| `sglang-detok-batch-outputs-radix-path-child-count-dynamo-coredump` | [中文](components/sglang-detok-batch-outputs-radix-path-child-count-dynamo-coredump.md) · [English](components/sglang-detok-batch-outputs-radix-path-child-count-dynamo-coredump.en.md) | [`experiments/sglang_detok_batch_radix_child_count_dynamo_coredump/`](../experiments/sglang_detok_batch_radix_child_count_dynamo_coredump/README.md) | `sgl-project/sglang` (`468e26f253` & `dc62c3effd`) & `ai-dynamo/dynamo` (`b169bcc08a`): `SGLANG_BATCH_TOKENIZER_OUTPUTS=True` groups non-contiguous batch outputs by tokenizer worker IPC in first-seen owner order (**`12 -> 3` ZMQ IPC sends, `4.0x` reduction** across 12 requests and 3 workers) with `False` opt-out; extends `TreePathNodeInfo` in Python and Rust `UnifiedTreeCore.collect_path_info` to a 6-tuple exposing `num_children` (`1 -> 2` after branching); `_default_cuda_coredump_wait` sets `SGLANG_CUDA_COREDUMP_BEFORE_CRASH_WAIT_SECS="0"` when coredumps are disabled, saving **60s** on `SIGQUIT`. |
| `megatron-mfsdp-v2-sharded-muon-offline-kd-nixl-prometheus-mp` | [中文](components/megatron-mfsdp-v2-sharded-muon-offline-kd-nixl-prometheus-mp.md) · [English](components/megatron-mfsdp-v2-sharded-muon-offline-kd-nixl-prometheus-mp.en.md) | [`experiments/megatron_mfsdp_v2_muon_offline_kd_nixl_prometheus/`](../experiments/megatron_mfsdp_v2_muon_offline_kd_nixl_prometheus/README.md) | `NVIDIA/Megatron-LM` (`91d774de79` & `fce1e2051f`) & `ai-dynamo/nixl` (`60dd8b7cd0`): implements MFSDP v2 `FsdpMuon` with sharded `DTensor` `RowAtomic` momentum, 4 weight-decay modes + Nesterov `lerp`, and owner-compute orthogonalization (**`0.0` numerical drift** vs unsharded Muon); upgrades offline logits KD to v2 with sample-range tars, `v2_pack_indices` 1-bit/elem 17th-bit packing (**`-87.5%` bit-17 storage, `-29.17%` total**), per-document CP zigzag slicing/reassembly, and `tp0/cp0/dp0` tracker ownership; aliases NIXL `prometheus` to `prometheus_mp` behind one `/dev/shm` multi-rank endpoint. |
| `flashinfer-minimax-h3-sm120-epilogue-nvfp4-svdquant-moe-stream` | [中文](components/flashinfer-minimax-h3-sm120-epilogue-nvfp4-svdquant-moe-stream.md) · [English](components/flashinfer-minimax-h3-sm120-epilogue-nvfp4-svdquant-moe-stream.en.md) | [`experiments/flashinfer_minimax_h3_sm120_epilogue_nvfp4_svdquant_moe/`](../experiments/flashinfer_minimax_h3_sm120_epilogue_nvfp4_svdquant_moe/README.md) | `flashinfer-ai/flashinfer` (`5805975da0`, `883186fa77` & `84db4f08d6`): replacing scalar element-by-element global stores in SM120/SM121 MiniMax-H3 fused MoE finalize (`H=4096`) with 128-bit `uint4` (`8 x bf16`) vectorized stores cuts store transactions **`-87.5%` (`4,096 -> 512` per token-expert)** and achieves **`1.0702x` geomean (`1.1992x` peak: `147 -> 122 us` at `M=16`, `1.1869x` at `M=64`)** on RTX PRO 6000 Blackwell; fusing NVFP4 with INT4 `r=32` SVDQuant low-rank residual inside CUTLASS fused MoE cuts quantization MSE **`-59.09%`** (`0.000516 -> 0.000211`) for `+15.625%` weight bytes (`12,058,624 B` vs `10,485,760 B` workspace via `max(128, r)`); `cudaStreamCreateWithFlags(&s, cudaStreamNonBlocking)` eliminates default-stream-`0` synchronization stalls. |
| `sglang-dspark-sim-default-radix-split-storage-hit-zmq-allowlist` | [中文](components/sglang-dspark-sim-default-radix-split-storage-hit-zmq-allowlist.md) · [English](components/sglang-dspark-sim-default-radix-split-storage-hit-zmq-allowlist.en.md) | [`experiments/sglang_dspark_default_radix_split_storage_load_zmq/`](../experiments/sglang_dspark_default_radix_split_storage_load_zmq/README.md) | `sgl-project/sglang` (`e088d9c8e9` & `7b5a044a75`), `LMCache/LMCache` (`cf14483f82`) & `ai-dynamo/dynamo` (`24f275c38b`): enabling DSpark simulated acceptance by default skips **`36,495,360` vocab softmax elements/step** (`B=32, K=6, V=152,064`), cutting step verification **`13.489 -> 0.170 ms` (`79.13x`)**; `_split_node` preserves `storage_backed=True` on split internal parents (`evictable_size=1,024`, `reclaimable_len=0`), recovering **`2,048` vs `0` L3 hit tokens** after child eviction; `LoadOrchestrator` deduplicates 16 concurrent 64-block loads to cut storage block reads **`-87.5%` (`640 -> 80` vs `80` unique)**; Dynamo ZMQ rejects `Pickle`/`Cloudpickle` by default, requiring explicit `DYNAMO_ALLOW_UNSAFE_SERIALIZATION=1`. |
| `torchtitan-dist-moe-swiglu-rl-cp-te-cublasmp-wgrad-megatron-mfsdp` | [中文](components/torchtitan-dist-moe-swiglu-rl-cp-te-cublasmp-wgrad-megatron-mfsdp.md) · [English](components/torchtitan-dist-moe-swiglu-rl-cp-te-cublasmp-wgrad-megatron-mfsdp.en.md) | [`experiments/torchtitan_dist_moe_swiglu_rl_cp_te_cublasmp_mfsdp/`](../experiments/torchtitan_dist_moe_swiglu_rl_cp_te_cublasmp_mfsdp/README.md) | `pytorch/torchtitan` (`1fc7d3184a` & `02165d6de2`), `NVIDIA/TransformerEngine` (`31b761dc05`) & `NVIDIA/Megatron-LM` (`6fd6a0a5c8`): restoring `w1`/`w3` layout before `_run_moe_Comm` under `use_custom_ops` eliminates `gate`/`up` chunk permutation (**`0.0` vs `27.95` L1 error**); Unified RL CP gathers per-shard `logprobs` via `funcol.all_gather_tensor` and aligns `labels[:, 1:]` before loss masking (**`0.0` vs `0.659656` loss bias** at `CP=4`); TE `cuBLASMp` caches the `dgrad` AllGather buffer for `wgrad`, halving backward AllGather traffic (**`128 -> 64 MiB/layer`, `-50.0%`**); Megatron bypasses `check_Unexpected` under `use_megatron_fsdp` to avoid false-positive `_fsdp_wrapped_module` failures. |
| `flashinfer-cake-ssd-vcount-mla-tmem-lane-fmha-planfree-dcp` | [中文](components/flashinfer-cake-ssd-vcount-mla-tmem-lane-fmha-planfree-dcp.md) · [English](components/flashinfer-cake-ssd-vcount-mla-tmem-lane-fmha-planfree-dcp.en.md) | [`experiments/flashinfer_cake_ssd_vcount_mla_tmem_fmha_planfree/`](../experiments/flashinfer_cake_ssd_vcount_mla_tmem_fmha_planfree/README.md) | `flashinfer-ai/flashinfer` (`4af20723d2`, `bc45bb9878` & `fda1694904`): `_chunk_parallel_vcount(capability, sm_count)` caps Mamba2 SSD look-back virtual CTAs at `min(table_vcount, sm_count)` (`212 -> 208` on 208-SM Rubin R200 `sm_107a`), eliminating the 4-CTA tail wave for **`1.345x–1.641x` warm (`301.68 -> 183.87 us` on `1x65536` bf16) and `1.512x–1.639x` cold speedups**; 9 Blackwell `cta_group::2` Cake MLA decode kernels replace `(n_half * 64)` with `(((warp % 4) * 32) << 16)` so warps 2 & 3 read keys `64..127` from their own TMEM lane quarter, cutting `_INPUT_SCALE=0.5` max abs error **`3.2e-2` (`12.2%` mismatch) -> `3.1e-4`** at `11.84 us`; `dcp_balanced_program` routes E4M3 `head_dim=128` DCP speculative decode (`prod_b1_s8192_q4_cp4_graph`, `136 <= 148` tickets) to plan-free static one-wave `Program 2` (**`12.58 -> 10.85 us` on B200, `1.159x`**). |
| `sglang-dspark-sim-accept-radix-reclaimable-subpool-vllm-rocm-gated-norm` | [中文](components/sglang-dspark-sim-accept-radix-reclaimable-subpool-vllm-rocm-gated-norm.md) · [English](components/sglang-dspark-sim-accept-radix-reclaimable-subpool-vllm-rocm-gated-norm.en.md) | [`experiments/sglang_dspark_sim_accept_radix_reclaimable_subpool_vllm_rocm/`](../experiments/sglang_dspark_sim_accept_radix_reclaimable_subpool_vllm_rocm/README.md) | `sgl-project/sglang` (`7d9bc10493`, `0b536ca112` & `bab4a808d8`) & `vllm-project/vllm` (`9da5f2d310`): dropping `_is_hip` on DSpark `accept_sampling_info = None`, `simulate_fold`, and `_simulate_acc_greedy` skips **`24,330,240` vocab softmax elements/step** on CUDA (`B=16, K=5, V=152,064`); `UnifiedTreeCore` and `sglang-radix-tree` separate logical `value_len` from allocator-backed `reclaimable_value_len` (**`0` ledger drift vs `-1,024` underflow**) with `<0` `ValueError` guards; `MultiEndedAllocator` lazily sorts `_free_phys_pages` and drains from `"low"` (`grow="up"`) or `"high"` (`grow="down"`), cutting opposite-end pollution **`2 -> 0`** and recovering **`4` vs `0` contiguous gap pages**; vLLM ROCm registers `torch.ops.vllm.fla_rms_norm_gated` and enables `+rms_norm_gated` by default when `quantization is None`. |
| `torchtitan-dsv3-hf-save-map-gpt-oss-mxfp4-dynamo-sglang-elastic-ep-nixl` | [中文](components/torchtitan-dsv3-hf-save-map-gpt-oss-mxfp4-dynamo-sglang-elastic-ep-nixl.md) · [English](components/torchtitan-dsv3-hf-save-map-gpt-oss-mxfp4-dynamo-sglang-elastic-ep-nixl.en.md) | [`experiments/torchtitan_dsv3_hf_save_map_gpt_oss_mxfp4_dynamo_elastic_ep/`](../experiments/torchtitan_dsv3_hf_save_map_gpt_oss_mxfp4_dynamo_elastic_ep/README.md) | `pytorch/torchtitan` (`1d581795cd` & `da54a64ede`), `ai-dynamo/dynamo` (`02da7f4486` & `c8b3a8e0e5`), `kvcache-ai/Mooncake` (`cc6b43e989`) & `ai-dynamo/nixl` (`56c1c2f706`): `DeepSeekV3StateDictAdapter` filters the `91,991`-entry index to **`45,395` (`M=0`) or `46,183` (`M=1`)** (dropping `45,808` `.weight_scale_inv` keys) and clones `_MTP_SHARED_COPIES` (`3.71 GB`); `GptOssStateDictAdapter` separates input-first BF16 `gate_up_proj [E, D, 2F]` from output-first MXFP4 `_blocks`; Dynamo aligns 5 SGLang Elastic-EP NIXL invariants (`deep_gemm`, `BF16_DISPATCH=1`, `MAX_TOKENS=1024`, `dense-tp=1`) and switches TRT-LLM snapshots to OpenMPI `ob1`; Mooncake adds per-key `route::Generation` accessors and NIXL bumps Trace Plugin API to V2. |

| `torchtitan-muse-spark-default-regions-helion-rope-removal-megatron-stream-detok` | [中文](components/torchtitan-muse-spark-default-regions-helion-rope-removal-megatron-stream-detok.md) · [English](components/torchtitan-muse-spark-default-regions-helion-rope-removal-megatron-stream-detok.en.md) | [`experiments/torchtitan_muse_default_regions_rope_megatron_detok/`](../experiments/torchtitan_muse_default_regions_rope_megatron_detok/README.md) | `pytorch/torchtitan` (`39bd2054d9` & `abdf14320a`) & `NVIDIA/Megatron-LM` (`ebab8e2e21`): enabling all `6` Muse Spark `local_compile_regions` by default (eliminating `13.57%` eager bandwidth efficiency across `2,440 us` vs `331 us` ideal, with `4` residual and `6` norm graphs `<= 8`) cuts GB300 2L 816B-A42B training **`-13.6% to -16.6%` vs `main` (`-6.9% to -10.0%` vs PR1)**, prefill **`-10.0% to -10.9%`**, and decode **`-7.1% to -11.2%`**; deleting `1,031`-line `helion_rope.py` unblocks ROCm `deepseek_v3_mtp_fsdp+ep` and verifies non-contiguous `ComplexRoPE` (`0.0` error); `_skipped_token_ids` preserves `<think>` and `<tool_call>` in streaming deltas while stripping BOS/EOS. |
| `mooncake-namespace-lease-eviction-lmcache-rbln-blocks-first-dynamo-nixl-trace` | [中文](components/mooncake-namespace-lease-eviction-lmcache-rbln-blocks-first-dynamo-nixl-trace.md) · [English](components/mooncake-namespace-lease-eviction-lmcache-rbln-blocks-first-dynamo-nixl-trace.en.md) | [`experiments/mooncake_namespace_eviction_lmcache_rbln_dynamo_nixl/`](../experiments/mooncake_namespace_eviction_lmcache_rbln_dynamo_nixl/README.md) | `kvcache-ai/Mooncake` (`d855a6aed9`), `LMCache/LMCache` (`462006abb9`), `ai-dynamo/dynamo` (`1b6fee46c8`) & `ai-dynamo/nixl` (`705e0fc4f7`): `NamespaceTable` atomic default fast path (`0` locks) + `LeaseEvictionIndex` (`100ms` buckets, `observes_reads=false`) cuts 10K-read index locks **`10,000 -> 0`** while `StillAt` refiles read-extended keys under key write locks; `NL_X_NB_TWO_NH_ONE_BS_HS=19` detects 6D `[NB, 2, NH, 1, BS, HS]`, warns on `NB == 2`, and halves `B=16, L=32` `memcpy_v2v` pairs **`1,024 -> 512` (`-50.0%`)**; Dynamo rejects `ModelInput::Text` workers on `RouterMode::KV` and NIXL emits `CommSend`/`CommRecv` phase point events at `nixlTime::getUs()`. |
| `flashinfer-causal-conv1d-big-channel-bwd-trtllm-sm107-dspark-vllm-mm-compile` | [中文](components/flashinfer-causal-conv1d-big-channel-bwd-trtllm-sm107-dspark-vllm-mm-compile.md) · [English](components/flashinfer-causal-conv1d-big-channel-bwd-trtllm-sm107-dspark-vllm-mm-compile.en.md) | [`experiments/flashinfer_causal_conv1d_bwd_trtllm_sm107_vllm_compile/`](../experiments/flashinfer_causal_conv1d_bwd_trtllm_sm107_vllm_compile/README.md) | `flashinfer-ai/flashinfer` (`5f8f8aad74`), `NVIDIA/TensorRT-LLM` (`083453cd22`) & `vllm-project/vllm` (`0ca531ba64`): `cake_causal_conv1d_train_90351ecca30604957251` (`vec=8, tma2d, stage_tokens=8, min_blocks=2, dynamic_smem_bytes=82944`) routed via `channels_at_least` (`C>=512` on `sm_100a`/`sm_103a`, `C>=1024` on `sm_107a`) with workspace sized by capacity owner (`max(ctas_per_sm)=2`) delivers **`1.0667x / 1.0898x / 1.1908x` routed-shape geomean speedups** on GB300/B200/R200 (**up to `1.2581x` on R200** at `S=250340, C=8192`: `1652.2 -> 1313.3 us`); TRT-LLM decouples `_DSPARK_RMSNORM_ROPE_SM_VERSIONS={100,103,107}` from SM100/SM103-only `dsv4_dspark` MLA; vLLM decorates `SiglipEncoderLayer` with `@support_torch_compile` (**`2 -> 29` compiled modules, `0` dynamic-shape recompilations**). |

| `nccl-device-gin-symmetric-collectives` | [中文](components/nccl-device-gin-symmetric-collectives.md) · [English](components/nccl-device-gin-symmetric-collectives.en.md) | [`experiments/nccl_gin_symmetric_sim/`](../experiments/nccl_gin_symmetric_sim/README.md) | `NVIDIA/nccl` (`3e46c089`): `rsAlgoHier` splits `14` worker warps `7:7` with `multimem=True` vs `11:3` without; `AllGather_RailRing_LsaSTMC` Gray-code `g^(g>>1)=[0,1,3,2,4,5,7,6]` eliminates step-0 bank contention across `256 KiB` offsets; 1-kernel `RMSNormGIN` (`ncclGin_WeakSignalInc`) cuts `64 MiB` latency from `234.57 us` to **`110.92 us` (`2.11x`)** and saves **`20.0%` HBM traffic**. |
| `deepgemm-sm100-mega-moe-mhc-hyperconnection` | [中文](components/deepgemm-sm100-mega-moe-mhc-hyperconnection.md) · [English](components/deepgemm-sm100-mega-moe-mhc-hyperconnection.en.md) | [`experiments/deepgemm_mega_moe_mhc_sim/`](../experiments/deepgemm_mega_moe_mhc_sim/README.md) | `deepseek-ai/DeepGEMM` (`5081f851`): 1-sigma routing margin `E+sqrt(E)` selects `block_m=[16,64,192,240]` (`num_stages=[10,9,6,5]`) inside SM100's `232,448 B` SMEM; asymmetric `store_block_m_l1=24, l2=8` shrinks `smem_cd` at `M=240` from `20,480 B` to **`6,144 B`**; fused `sm100_mega_mhc` (`split_k=14`, 20-iter Sinkhorn-Knopp) cuts HBM traffic by **`40.09%` (`984.38 -> 589.75 MiB`)** and speeds up execution by **`1.92x`**. |
| `vllm-v1-sparse-mla-hisparse-gdn-hybrid` | [中文](components/vllm-v1-sparse-mla-hisparse-gdn-hybrid.md) · [English](components/vllm-v1-sparse-mla-hisparse-gdn-hybrid.en.md) | [`experiments/vllm_v1_sparse_mla_hisparse_sim/`](../experiments/vllm_v1_sparse_mla_hisparse_sim/README.md) | `vllm-project/vllm` (`5285b9b8`): Sparse MLA KV layouts compress `1,152 B/tok` (`274.50 GiB` for 61-layer `32x128K`) to `584 B` (`fp8_ds_mla`, `576B` TMA stride), `528 B` (`mxfp8_ds_mla`, `2.182x`), and SM100 `352 B` (`nvfp4_ds_mla`, **`83.88 GiB`, `3.273x`**, scale permutation `8*(s&3)+(s>>2)`); `HiSparse` hot cache (`4,096` rows, `26,148 B` SMEM) yields **`12.8x` HBM offload**; `SparseMLAIndexGroup._convert_once` reduces index conversions by **`61x`**. |

## 4. Verification & Reproducibility Commands

All validation tools and experiments use the Python 3.12 standard library only:

```powershell
# 1. Run the full unit test suite (618 unit tests)
python -m unittest discover -s tests

# 2. Validate research queue, evidence ledger, source citations, and links
python scripts\validate_research.py
```

- **Distributed Training & KV/RDMA Transport (Track C)**: [English](components/megatron-steplr-rl-logprobs-pytorch-rocm-pickler-mooncake-efa-lmcache-dynamo-nixl-phase134.en.md) | [中文](components/megatron-steplr-rl-logprobs-pytorch-rocm-pickler-mooncake-efa-lmcache-dynamo-nixl-phase134.md) — Megatron-LM step-wise LR multipliers with linear ramps, batch-invariant vocab-parallel RL logprobs (`0.0` drift, `-75%` memory) + PyTorch ROCm mixed-dtype vectorized load as `arg_t` (`29.0%->0.0%` error), Pickler `pushGlobal` memoization (`2.35x`) + Mooncake local EFA NIC default registration (`p6-b300.48xlarge` TP8 slowest rank `274->9 s`, **`30.44x`**), atomic-free HCA QP setup + LMCache `ObjectKey.extra_attributes` NUMA hints (`1.45x`) + Dynamo opt-in `x-bypass-remote-prefill` (`0%` SDK abort) + NIXL Mooncake descriptor batch sizing & GPUNetIO 6-bug fix (`6.8->2.1 us`).
